Qoder 是面向真实工作的智能体平台，通过多智能体协同、长时委派执行、记忆与知识引擎，连接代码库与工具链，覆盖从任务理解、规划、执行、验证到交付的完整流程。

每位参赛选手可领取 2000 Qoder CN Credits，相当于 1 个月 Pro 权益，并可使用 Qoder CN 的完整产品能力开发参赛作品。

| 项目 | 说明 |
| :--- | :--- |
| 项目 | Qoder × OceanBase |
| 题目 | Build with Qoder——用 Qoder 做一个让学习、科研和工作更高效的项目 |
| 任务 | 围绕工作提效或学习科研，使用 Qoder 开发一个项目，结合 seekdb 或 PowerContext 实现核心功能，最后通过 Qoder Sites 发布项目网站 |
| 技术要求 | 项目必须使用 Qoder 开发，并实际使用 seekdb、PowerContext 中的至少一种；项目网站必须通过 Qoder Sites 发布 |
| 基础要求 | 评审可通过项目网站了解项目，按运行说明跑通主要功能，并核对 Qoder 开发过程和指定技术的实际作用 |
| 提交产物 | 项目网站网址、代码仓库、运行说明、验证样例、Qoder 开发记录 |
| 评价方式 | 基础验收关注可运行性、Qoder 使用和技术结合；作品评价关注项目的实用价值、完成度、技术实现和真实使用效果，具体评分细则及权重由赛事后续公布 |
| 总分 | 20 |


## 一、题目背景
整理文献要翻几十篇论文，复习时找不到上学期的笔记，周报总要从聊天记录里一条条拼。学习、科研和工作中，总有一些重复又耗时的环节值得被改造。

Qoder 是面向真实开发工作的智能体编程平台。开发者用自然语言描述需求，Qoder 可以完成需求拆解、编码、调试和部署。

本题以工作提效和学习科研为主题。参赛者可以从自己或身边同学、同事的一个真实痛点出发，用 Qoder 开发项目解决它，并结合 seekdb 或 PowerContext 实现核心功能。项目完成后，参赛者使用 Qoder Sites 发布项目网站，作为作品展示和评审体验的入口。项目形式不限，应用、工具、插件、Agent 均可。

## 二、题目内容
### 2.1 用 Qoder 开发项目
项目的主要开发工作必须在 Qoder 中完成。桌面端、IDE、JetBrains 插件和 CLI 均可使用。

参赛者需保留开发过程记录，说明 Qoder 在需求拆解、编码、调试等环节中完成了哪些工作。仅用 Qoder 发布网站而项目在其他工具中开发的作品，不符合本赛道要求。

### 2.2 技术结合要求
| 组成 | 要求与作用 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Qoder（必选） | 承担项目的主要开发工作，并通过 Qoder Sites 发布项目网站 |
| seekdb（至少选择一种） | 为项目提供数据存储、查询与检索能力，例如知识库检索、内容匹配或业务数据查询 |
| PowerContext（至少选择一种） | 为项目提供记忆与上下文能力，例如保存用户偏好、任务进展或历史互动，并在后续使用中复用 |


seekdb 支持向量、全文和结构化条件结合的混合检索，提供嵌入式、服务端和 Docker 等部署方式，兼容 MySQL 协议，参见 [seekdb 项目说明](https://github.com/oceanbase/seekdb/blob/master/README_CN.md)。PowerContext 是面向 AI Agent 的开源上下文服务，支持 HTTP API、MCP 和 Python SDK 接入，参见 [PowerContext 项目说明](https://github.com/oceanbase/powercontext/blob/master/README_CN.md)。同时使用两者的作品应说明各自分工。

指定技术必须在项目功能中实际发挥作用。参赛者应展示数据或检索结果如何用于项目流程，或记忆如何被保存并在后续使用中复用。仅安装组件或测试连接，不满足这一要求。

### 2.3 用 Qoder Sites 发布项目网站
项目完成后，在 Qoder 中通过 Sites 创建并发布项目网站，并将可见范围设置为「任何人都可访问」。企业版账号无法设置公开访问，请使用个人账号发布。

项目网站应包含项目简介、解决的问题、核心功能、演示视频或截图、代码仓库链接，以及体验入口（如有）。有使用效果数据的项目，建议在网站上一并展示。项目网站用于展示作品和提供评审入口，评审对象是项目本身，网站不单独评分。

### 2.4 使用效果（鼓励提交）
本题鼓励参赛者把项目交给真实用户使用，并记录使用效果。效果数据不是基础验收的必要条件，但会作为评价维度之一。

| 内容 | 示例 |
| :--- | :--- |
| 使用规模 | 有多少同学或同事使用过，累计使用次数，持续使用了多长时间 |
| 提效结果 | 使用前后的对比，例如整理一次文献从 2 小时缩短到 20 分钟 |
| 用户反馈 | 问卷结果、用户评价截图、收到的改进建议，以及据此做出的迭代 |


数据须真实可核对，并附上来源，例如访问统计截图、使用日志、问卷导出结果。项目网站部署在 Qoder Sites 上时，可在站点的「访问统计」中查看访问次数和独立访客。展示用户反馈时，请隐去姓名、联系方式等个人信息。

### 2.5 参考方向
以下只是示例，欢迎从自己的真实需求出发设计项目。

| 方向 | 示例 |
| :--- | :--- |
| 文献与资料检索 | 把论文、课程笔记或实验记录导入 seekdb，按语义和关键词混合检索，快速找到相关段落和出处 |
| 学习助手 | 用 PowerContext 记住每个人的薄弱知识点和复习进度，做一个越用越懂你的复习教练 |
| 科研流程工具 | 自动整理实验数据、生成组会汇报初稿，或追踪领域内的新论文并按研究方向推送 |
| 办公提效 | 汇总会议纪要、聊天记录和待办，自动生成周报；或用 seekdb 搭一个团队内部的文档问答助手 |
| 多 Agent 协作 | 让多个 Agent 通过 PowerContext 共享任务上下文，分工完成调研、写作或数据整理 |


## 三、验收标准
### 3.1 基础验收
满足以下条件，即达到基础要求：

1. **网站可访问。** 项目网站通过 Qoder Sites 发布，未登录状态下也能打开。
2. **项目能够运行。** 评审按运行说明完成配置后，项目能够启动，并可按验证样例体验主要功能。
3. **Qoder 开发可核对。** 开发记录能够体现项目的主要功能由 Qoder 参与完成。
4. **技术结合有效。** seekdb 或 PowerContext 在项目功能中实际发挥作用，可通过查询结果、检索记录或记忆读写记录核对。

截图、录屏和运行日志可作为验证材料，但仍需提供可运行的项目和明确的复现步骤。

### 3.2 评价维度
| 维度 | 关注内容 |
| :--- | :--- |
| 实用价值 | 项目是否针对工作或学习科研中的真实痛点，解决方式是否有效 |
| 使用效果 | 是否有真实用户使用，提效结果和用户反馈是否有可核对的数据支撑 |
| 完成度与体验 | 主要流程是否完整、顺畅，项目是否实现了所描述的功能 |
| 技术结合 | seekdb、PowerContext 的使用是否合理，是否对项目功能产生实际帮助 |
| Qoder 开发实践 | 开发记录是否清楚展示了 Qoder 在项目中的作用 |
| 工程质量 | 代码结构是否清晰，依赖与配置是否齐全，评审能否顺利复现 |


具体评分细则、权重及评测环境由赛事后续公布。

## 四、提交材料
| 材料 | 要求 |
| :--- | :--- |
| 项目网站网址 | Qoder Sites 发布后的完整访问网址，可见范围为「任何人都可访问」 |
| 代码仓库 | 完整项目源码，公开仓库或向评审开放访问权限 |
| 运行说明 | 运行环境、依赖版本、seekdb 或 PowerContext 的部署与接入方式、从安装到启动的完整步骤 |
| 验证样例 | 至少一组可按步骤执行的样例，包含初始数据、输入、操作步骤、预期结果，以及指定技术发挥作用的记录 |
| Qoder 开发记录 | 关键开发环节的 Qoder 对话截图或录屏，并附 Qoder 账号 UID，用于核对站点归属及使用记录 |
| 使用效果材料（鼓励提交） | 使用人数、使用次数、提效对比和用户反馈，并附访问统计截图、使用日志或问卷结果等来源 |


配置示例使用占位值，不提交真实 API Key、密码或其他凭据。复用开源代码或数据时，注明来源及所作改动。

## 五、提交方式
1. 登录大赛测试平台，进入 Qoder 赛题的提交入口。
2. 填写项目网站网址、代码仓库地址和 Qoder 账号 UID，并上传其余材料。
3. 提交前用浏览器无痕窗口打开项目网站，确认可以正常访问。

完整流程为：用 Qoder 开发项目 → 接入 seekdb 或 PowerContext → 用 Qoder Sites 发布项目网站 → 整理提交材料 → 提交到测试平台。

评审结束前，请勿暂停或删除已提交的站点。发布后修改网站内容，需要再次点击「发布」才会更新线上版本。

# 赛题分析与 Idea 建议

## 赛题核心约束

- **必选**：Qoder 开发 + Qoder Sites 发布
- **至少选一种**：seekdb（向量/全文混合检索）或 PowerContext（记忆与上下文）
- **主题**：学习/科研/工作中的真实痛点
- **评审重点**：实用价值 > 使用效果 > 完成度 > 技术结合深度

---

## Idea 1：「论文脉络图」—— 文献综述智能助手

**痛点**：写 related work 时要翻几十篇论文，理清谁引用谁、谁改进了谁的方法，手动整理极其耗时。

**方案**：
- 用户上传论文 PDF，提取摘要/方法/结论，embedding 后存入 **seekdb**
- 用 seekdb 的向量检索做语义相似度匹配，自动发现"方法相似但互不引用"的论文关联
- **PowerContext** 记住用户的研究方向、已读论文、关注点，下次上传新论文时自动关联已有文献库
- 输出：按主题聚类的文献脉络图 + 每篇论文的一句话贡献总结

**技术分工**：seekdb 做论文语义检索与聚类；PowerContext 做用户研究画像与阅读进度持久化。

---

## Idea 2：「渐进式刷题教练」—— 越用越懂你的复习系统

**痛点**：考前复习不知道哪些知识点薄弱，传统 flashcard 工具千人一面的复习计划效率低。

**方案**：
- 用户导入课程笔记/题库，seekdb 做知识点级别的语义检索
- 每次练习后，**PowerContext** 记录每个知识点的掌握程度、错误模式、复习频率
- 系统根据记忆曲线 + 用户薄弱点动态生成复习计划，优先推薄弱知识点
- 支持自然语言提问："我下周考数据库，帮我复习"，从 seekdb 检索相关题目，按 PowerContext 中的薄弱点排序

**技术分工**：seekdb 做题目/笔记检索；PowerContext 做用户知识掌握状态建模（长期记忆）。

---

## Idea 3：「会议纪要 → 周报自动生成器」

**痛点**：每周写周报要从聊天记录、会议笔记、Git log 里一条条拼，重复且容易遗漏。

**方案**：
- 用户随时丢入碎片信息（会议记录、聊天截图、TODO），seekdb 按语义存储并做全文+向量混合检索
- **PowerContext** 记住用户的团队成员、项目归属、周报格式偏好
- 周五一键生成：按项目维度聚合本周所有碎片信息，自动分类为"已完成/进行中/待办"，生成周报初稿
- 支持追问："上周和张三讨论的部署方案后来怎样了？"，从 seekdb 检索相关上下文

**技术分工**：seekdb 做碎片信息的语义检索与聚合；PowerContext 做用户偏好、团队关系、格式记忆。

---

## Idea 4：「实验记录本」—— 科研实验可追溯知识库

**痛点**：实验参数、结果、失败原因散落在 notebook、聊天记录、纸质笔记里，复现实验时找不到当时的配置。

**方案**：
- 每次实验记录（参数、结果、分析）embedding 入 **seekdb**，支持按实验条件/结果/日期混合检索
- **PowerContext** 记住当前实验项目的目标、进行中实验的状态、常用参数范围
- 自然语言查询："上次用 learning rate 0.01 的实验结果怎样？" 或 "哪些实验的准确率超过了 90%？"
- 自动生成实验对比表，辅助组会汇报

**技术分工**：seekdb 做实验记录的语义检索；PowerContext 做实验项目状态追踪。

---

## Idea 5：「团队文档问答 Bot」—— 企业内部知识助手

**痛点**：团队文档散落在 Confluence、飞书、本地 wiki，新人入职找不到信息，老人也记不住细节。

**方案**：
- 将团队文档（Markdown/PDF/HTML）批量导入 **seekdb**，建立向量+全文索引
- **PowerContext** 记住每个用户的角色（新人/开发/PM）、常问领域、历史提问
- 用户提问后，seekdb 检索相关文档段落，PowerContext 提供个性化上下文（"这个用户是新人，回答要更详细"）
- 支持追问和多轮对话，答案附带文档出处链接

**技术分工**：seekdb 做文档检索（核心）；PowerContext 做用户画像与个性化回答。

---

## 推荐排序

| 排名 | Idea | 理由 |
|:---:|:---|:---|
| 1 | 渐进式刷题教练 | 痛点普遍、技术结合深、容易做出"越用越好"的演示效果 |
| 2 | 周报自动生成器 | 打工人共鸣强、演示直观、两项技术分工清晰 |
| 3 | 论文脉络图 | 科研场景契合度高、seekdb 向量检索价值突出 |
| 4 | 团队文档问答 Bot | 实现相对简单但差异化不足 |
| 5 | 实验记录本 | 受众偏窄但技术匹配好 |

## Report: Repository Exploration Results

### 2. PowerContext Project (`C:\Users\ouyan\Desktop\oceanbase\powercontext`)

**Status: A mature, full-featured open-source project (v1.4.1)** by OceanBase, Apache 2.0 licensed.

#### What PowerContext Provides

PowerContext is an **open-source context/memory service for AI agents**. Its core value proposition:

- **Memory** -- revisioned, durable decisions/constraints/facts that survive across sessions and agent switches. Stored as `atomic-memory/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION`.
- **Handoff** -- structured work continuity (Work Contract -> Prepared Handoff -> Acknowledgement -> Task Outcome).
- **Experience** -- situation/action/outcome/lesson records that go through a review inbox before reuse.
- **Skill** -- reviewed, versioned procedures that can be exported to agents (Codex, Claude Code, etc.).
- **Source** -- evidence journal (prompts, documents, task outcomes).
- **Topic Memory** -- auto-extracted from Sources in the background.
- **PreparedContext** -- bounded recall with a byte budget, combining active Memory + approved Experience.

Benchmark results: 90.78% on LoCoMo (vs 52.9% full-context replay), +4.38 points on SWE-bench Pro.

#### How to Integrate (4 interfaces)

| Interface | Description | Entry Point |
|---|---|---|
| **HTTP API** | Complete remote contract, language-neutral. Default `http://127.0.0.1:8000`. OpenAPI spec at `/openapi.json`, Scalar docs at `/docs`. | `C:\Users\ouyan\Desktop\oceanbase\powercontext\openapi\powercontext.yaml` |
| **MCP** | Streamable HTTP at `/mcp`. Curated agent-facing tools for Source capture, Memory, Handoff, Review. | Enabled by default on the Server |
| **Python Client SDK** | Typed async client (`PowerContextClient`). Methods like `remember_memory`, `search_memory`, `prepare_context`, `create_source`, `create_artifact`. | `powercontext` PyPI package |
| **Core SDK** | In-process Python protocols for embedding directly. | `powercontext` base package |

#### Key HTTP API Endpoints

- `POST /v1/scopes` -- create a Scope
- `POST /v1/memory/remember` -- store a Memory entry
- `POST /v1/atomic-memory/search` -- search active memories
- `POST /v1/atomic-memory/list` -- list memory heads
- `POST /v1/context/prepare` -- get bounded PreparedContext
- `POST /v1/sources` -- capture Source evidence
- Unified artifact CRUD via `create_artifact`, `get_artifact`, `list_artifacts`, `replace_artifact`
- Candidate review: `/v1/candidates/list`, `get`, `history`, `revise`, `approve`, `reject`

#### Quick Start (minimal, no model API needed)

```bash
uv tool install --force "powercontext[cli,server]==1.4.1"
powercontext config init --language en --output .env
powercontext server run --env-file .env
```

#### Examples Available

## 「实验记录本」技术方案

### 系统架构

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Frontend (Next.js) → 部署到 Qoder Sites             │
│  ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌────────┐ │
│  │ 记录实验  │ │ 语义搜索  │ │ 项目看板  │ │ 对比表  │ │
│  └────┬─────┘ └────┬─────┘ └────┬─────┘ └───┬────┘ │
└───────┼─────────────┼────────────┼────────────┼──────┘
        │             │            │            │
        ▼             ▼            ▼            ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Backend (Python FastAPI)                            │
│  /api/experiments  /api/search  /api/projects        │
└────────┬──────────────────────────────┬──────────────┘
         │                              │
    ┌────▼────┐                   ┌─────▼─────┐
    │ seekdb  │                   │PowerContext│
    │ 向量检索 │                   │ 项目记忆    │
    │ 全文搜索 │                   │ 状态追踪    │
    └─────────┘                   └───────────┘
```

### 技术分工

| 组件 | 职责 |
|:---|:---|
| **seekdb** | 存储实验记录 embedding；支持自然语言语义检索（"上次跑 batch=32 的实验结果如何"）；全文检索实验标题/标签 |
| **PowerContext** | 管理科研项目状态（进行中/已完成/搁置）；记住用户的研究偏好和常用参数范围；追踪实验进展和待办事项 |

### 核心功能（MVP）

1. **记录实验** — 填写项目名、参数配置、结果指标、分析笔记
2. **语义搜索** — 自然语言查询实验历史，seekdb 做向量+全文混合检索
3. **项目追踪** — PowerContext 记住每个项目的目标、当前状态、关键决策
4. **实验对比** — 选多个实验，自动生成参数/结果对比表
5. **智能摘要** — 基于 PowerContext 中的项目状态，生成"本周实验进展"总结

### 数据模型

```
Experiment {
  id: uuid
  project_id: str          # 关联到 PowerContext scope
  title: str
  date: datetime
  parameters: json         # {"learning_rate": 0.01, "batch_size": 32, ...}
  metrics: json            # {"accuracy": 0.92, "loss": 0.15, ...}
  notes: text              # 自由文本，embedding 后入 seekdb
  tags: list[str]
  status: enum             # success / failed / inconclusive
}

Project (PowerContext Memory) {
  name: str
  goal: str
  status: active | paused | completed
  key_decisions: list      # 存在 PowerContext 中
  preferences: json        # 常用参数范围、关注指标
}
```

### 技术栈

| 层 | 选型 | 理由 |
|:---|:---|:---|
| 前端 | Next.js + Tailwind CSS | Qoder Sites 原生支持 |
| 后端 | Python FastAPI | 与 PowerContext SDK 同生态 |
| 向量检索 | seekdb (embedded) | 支持混合检索，部署简单 |
| 记忆/上下文 | PowerContext (HTTP API) | 项目状态持久化 |
| 部署 | Qoder Sites | 赛题要求 |

### 实现步骤

```
Phase 1: 后端核心（1-2天）
  ├── 初始化 FastAPI 项目
  ├── 接入 seekdb：实验记录 embedding + 检索
  ├── 接入 PowerContext：项目 CRUD + 状态管理
  └── 实现 API 端点

Phase 2: 前端界面（2-3天）
  ├── 实验记录表单
  ├── 搜索结果展示（高亮匹配段落）
  ├── 项目看板视图
  └── 实验对比表

Phase 3: 集成部署（1天）
  ├── 前后端联调
  ├── 部署到 Qoder Sites
  └── 准备验证样例
```

### 验证样例

> **场景**：用户跑了 5 组不同超参数的实验，想找到最优配置
>
> 1. 依次录入 5 条实验记录（不同 lr / batch_size / epochs）
> 2. 搜索："哪些实验的准确率超过 90%？" → seekdb 返回匹配结果
> 3. 选中 3 个实验生成对比表 → 直观看到参数与结果的关系
> 4. 查看项目状态 → PowerContext 返回当前项目进度和关键决策记录
> 5. 下次登录后，系统仍记得该项目的目标和偏好 → 验证记忆持久化

### 2. PowerContext Project (`C:\Users\ouyan\Desktop\oceanbase\powercontext`)

**Status: A mature, full-featured open-source project (v1.4.1)** by OceanBase, Apache 2.0 licensed.

#### What PowerContext Provides

PowerContext is an **open-source context/memory service for AI agents**. Its core value proposition:

- **Memory** -- revisioned, durable decisions/constraints/facts that survive across sessions and agent switches. Stored as `atomic-memory/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION`.
- **Handoff** -- structured work continuity (Work Contract -> Prepared Handoff -> Acknowledgement -> Task Outcome).
- **Experience** -- situation/action/outcome/lesson records that go through a review inbox before reuse.
- **Skill** -- reviewed, versioned procedures that can be exported to agents (Codex, Claude Code, etc.).
- **Source** -- evidence journal (prompts, documents, task outcomes).
- **Topic Memory** -- auto-extracted from Sources in the background.
- **PreparedContext** -- bounded recall with a byte budget, combining active Memory + approved Experience.

Benchmark results: 90.78% on LoCoMo (vs 52.9% full-context replay), +4.38 points on SWE-bench Pro.

#### How to Integrate (4 interfaces)

| Interface | Description | Entry Point |
|---|---|---|
| **HTTP API** | Complete remote contract, language-neutral. Default `http://127.0.0.1:8000`. OpenAPI spec at `/openapi.json`, Scalar docs at `/docs`. | `C:\Users\ouyan\Desktop\oceanbase\powercontext\openapi\powercontext.yaml` |
| **MCP** | Streamable HTTP at `/mcp`. Curated agent-facing tools for Source capture, Memory, Handoff, Review. | Enabled by default on the Server |
| **Python Client SDK** | Typed async client (`PowerContextClient`). Methods like `remember_memory`, `search_memory`, `prepare_context`, `create_source`, `create_artifact`. | `powercontext` PyPI package |
| **Core SDK** | In-process Python protocols for embedding directly. | `powercontext` base package |

#### Key HTTP API Endpoints

- `POST /v1/scopes` -- create a Scope
- `POST /v1/memory/remember` -- store a Memory entry
- `POST /v1/atomic-memory/search` -- search active memories
- `POST /v1/atomic-memory/list` -- list memory heads
- `POST /v1/context/prepare` -- get bounded PreparedContext
- `POST /v1/sources` -- capture Source evidence
- Unified artifact CRUD via `create_artifact`, `get_artifact`, `list_artifacts`, `replace_artifact`
- Candidate review: `/v1/candidates/list`, `get`, `history`, `revise`, `approve`, `reject`

#### Quick Start (minimal, no model API needed)

```bash
uv tool install --force "powercontext[cli,server]==1.4.1"
powercontext config init --language en --output .env
powercontext server run --env-file .env
```

#### Examples Available

- 08-11: Auto-extraction, search comparison, agent memory, HTTP API lifecycle
- 12-22: Advanced (team sharing, access control, source ingestion, background learning, MCP, dashboard, observability, full team workflow)
- A1: Custom components

**Other examples:**
- `examples/openai_sdk/` -- OpenAI SDK integration example
- `examples/systemone/` -- a complete system example with applicability evaluation

#### Storage Backends
- **SQLite** (default, simplest)
- **OceanBase** (MySQL mode) -- supported, with dedicated notebook verification
- **seekdb** (embedded, for when you need its specific capabilities)

1	# PowerContext
2	
3	**Context that stays with the work — across sessions, agents, and people.**
4	
5	[![PyPI version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/powercontext)](https://pypi.org/project/powercontext/)
6	[![License Apache 2.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-Apache_2.0-blue.svg)](LICENSE)

8	[![Ask DeepWiki](https://img.shields.io/badge/Ask-DeepWiki-147DFF)](https://deepwiki.com/oceanbase/powercontext)
9	
10	*[English](README.md) · [中文](README_CN.md) · [日本語](README_JP.md)*
11	
12	[Website](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/) · [Quickstart](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/docs/get-started/quickstart/) · [Manual startup](docs/en/docs/get-started/manual-start.md) · [Integrations](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/docs/integrations/) · [Benchmarks](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/benchmarks/) · [Interfaces](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/docs/develop/interfaces/)
13	
14	## What is PowerContext?
15	
16	**PowerContext is an open-source context service for AI agents.** It keeps the durable part of your work — decisions,
17	constraints, evidence, progress — outside any single conversation, so the next session, the next agent, or the next
18	person can pick up from the current state instead of rereading the transcript.
19	
20	You can use it as a ready-to-run Server or embed the same contracts in another application:
21	
22	| Layer | What it owns | Use it when |
23	| --- | --- | --- |
24	| Core protocol | `Source`, `Artifact`, and `Trigger` contracts | Embedding PowerContext or adding a component |
25	| Builtin runtime | Memory, Handoff, Experience, and Skill services, scheduling, inference, and supported storage backends | Running the standard local Server |
26	| Server and integrations | HTTP/MCP, Python Client, CLI, Dashboard, and Agent plugins | Connecting an Agent or application |
27	
28	Work rarely ends with whoever starts it. You hand a task to an agent, the agent gets part of the way, and later you or
29	someone else takes over. The reasoning and current state often stay behind in that conversation. PowerContext moves them
30	out of the conversation and into revisioned artifacts you can inspect:
31	
32	![You and agents hand work off and continue with stored context](docs/assets/readme-workflow.svg)
33	
34	Everything belongs to a **Scope** — one isolated Source journal, Memory lifecycle, review inbox, and Handoff history.
35	A Scope ID selects data; it does not prove user identity, grant tool access, or authorize execution.
36	
37	```text
38	Scope
39	├── Source journal    # evidence: prompts, documents, work contracts, task outcomes
40	├── Memory            # atomic-memory/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION — decisions, constraints, next steps
41	├── Review Inbox      # pending Experience and Skill candidates awaiting a decision
42	├── Handoff history   # handoff/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION — work continuity across sessions
43	└── Topic Memory      # extracted in the background from eligible Sources
44	```
45	
46	*The Dashboard screenshots below use synthetic demonstration records.*
47	
48	### Memory — decisions and constraints that outlive a session
49	
50	Memory is a revisioned Artifact family for reusable decisions, constraints, facts, state, and next steps. An entry is
51	`active`, `forgotten`, `merged`, or `retired`. Forgetting one through `POST /v1/atomic-memory/lifecycle` takes it out of
52	active recall while its content and history stay available and restorable, without advancing the content revision.
53	Explicit Memory writes need no model at all; extraction from captured Sources needs a configured generation pipeline.
54	
55	> "Don't refactor the old auth module — mobile is still using it."
56	> Context this expensive shouldn't depend on someone repeating it in every session.
57	
58	![Dashboard showing active decisions and constraints for one Scope with their Artifact and Source references](docs/assets/readme-dashboard-memories.png)
59	
60	### Handoff — the current state, not the whole transcript
61	
62	Handoff records work continuity as a four-step loop:
63	
64	```text
65	Work Contract → Prepared Handoff → Acknowledgement → Task Outcome
66	```
67	
68	`handoff_current_work` captures an inspected boundary and returns a temporary Prepared Handoff. Committing it creates a
69	durable Revision only when the user wants a milestone. The receiver resolves the Handoff and records an Acknowledgement;
70	a Task Outcome preserves the final status and checks as Source evidence. The
71	[Handoff Report](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/docs/workflows/use-handoff-report/) projects the latest Revision in
72	each selected Scope — read-only, and it never rewrites Memory.
73	
74	![Dashboard showing a committed Handoff Revision with recorded progress, next action, and linked source evidence](docs/assets/readme-dashboard-handoff.png)
75	
76	### Experience — what worked, reviewed before it is reused
77	
78	An Experience records a situation, an action, an observed outcome, and a reusable lesson. It enters a scope-local Review
79	Inbox as a pending Candidate. Review writes use `expected_version`, so a decision cannot silently apply after another
80	writer changes the proposal. Approval writes an immutable Revision; rejection records a reason. Both are terminal, and
81	only the approved current head takes part in recall — pending, rejected, and historical Revisions are excluded.
82	
83	### Skill — a reviewed procedure, exported on purpose
84	
85	A managed Skill holds a name, a discovery description, instructions, validation checks, and lineage. Approval does not
86	install or execute it: an exact approved Revision must be exported explicitly before an Agent host can discover the
87	host-local projection.
88	
89	[Operational metrics](docs/en/docs/operate/observability.md) expose dependency observations, processing freshness, and attempt latency, including a protected metrics-only endpoint for standalone background deployments.
90	
91	## Why PowerContext?
92	
93	- **Context lives outside the conversation.** Memory, Handoff, Experience, and Skill are server-side artifacts, not
94	  files inside one agent's home directory. Switch from Codex to Claude Code — or hand the work to a teammate — and the
95	  work travels with you.
96	- **Everything is revisioned and traceable.** Each Artifact is addressed as `FAMILY/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION`. The Artifact
97	  ID stays stable while approved replacements create later Revisions, and reading an exact reference returns that
98	  historical snapshot even after the family head advances.
99	- **Generation, review, and execution are separate steps.** A model proposes content, review approves a Revision, and
100	  export creates a host-local copy. None of those steps grants an Agent permission to execute instructions. See
101	  [Interfaces](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/docs/develop/interfaces/) for what each surface exposes today.
102	- **Recall is bounded, not dumped.** `PreparedContext` selects configured sections from active Memory and approved
103	  Experience, with optional Profile snapshots and Topic Memory, then applies a shared byte budget and returns `ready`
104	  content or `empty`. Recalled content is history, not an instruction authority: the receiving Agent still follows
105	  current instructions and checks live workspace state.
106	- **Review before reuse.** Experience and Skill pass through a review decision that is terminal either way, and
107	  approval writes an immutable Revision. With access control enforced, approval requires `scope.review`. An
108	  authorized Agent can approve its own Candidate;
109	  [separate submission and review](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/docs/workflows/review-candidates/#separate-agent-submission-from-review)
110	  with distinct identities and permissions when a person must decide.
111	- **One Server, several views of the same contract.** HTTP is the complete remote application contract. The Python Client
112	  gives typed access, MCP is a curated Agent-facing projection, and Core protocols let an application assemble its own
113	  Source, Artifact, and Trigger implementations.
114	
115	### How it compares
116	
117	The comparison assumes typical single-agent or host-local setups; exact behavior depends on how each system is deployed.
118	
119	| | Re-reading chat history | RAG over transcripts | Host-local memory files | PowerContext |
120	|---|---|---|---|---|
121	| Survives a new session | manual | yes | yes | yes |
122	| Survives switching agents | no | no | no | yes |
123	| Handing work to a person | manual | manual | manual | Handoff, first-class |
124	| Reusable procedure | no | no | prose only | Experience / Skill |
125	| Earlier revisions kept | no | depends on the index | only via git | immutable Revisions |
126	| Review before reuse | n/a | no | no | Review Inbox |
127	| Bounded recall for one turn | no | partial | no | `PreparedContext` byte budget |
128	| Inspectable outside the Agent | no | no | yes | yes |
129	
130	## Proof it works
131	
132	![Compact comparison of PowerContext results on LoCoMo and SWE-bench Pro](docs/assets/readme-benchmark-summary.svg)
133	
134	- **Long-conversation memory (LoCoMo; 1,540 scored questions across categories 1–4): 90.78% accuracy**, against 87.79%
135	  for PowerMem and 52.9% for full-context replay — **+37.88 points** over full context.
136	- **Coding tasks (SWE-bench Pro; a paired project run on all 731 public v2 tasks, not an official submission): 86.73%
137	  task resolution with PowerContext on, 82.35% with it off** — **+4.38 points** and 32 more tasks resolved.
138	- **Retrieval quality (LoCoMo Plus)**, native PowerContext against PowerContext with Jev candidate filtering after
139	  retrieval. Each model is used for both Memory extraction and answering; embeddings use `qwen3.7-text-embedding`
140	  (1024 dimensions):
141	
142	  | Model | PowerContext | PowerContext + Jev | Difference |
143	  |---|---|---|---|
144	  | GPT-4o-mini | 40.855% | 49.78% | +8.925 points |
145	  | Qwen3.7-plus | 60.37% | 68.947% | +8.577 points |
146	  | GPT-4o | 65.082% | 69.28% | +4.198 points |
147	
148	Full methods, results, and limitations: [benchmarks](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/benchmarks/). Two caveats we
149	would rather state than bury: the six LoCoMo Plus scores above were supplied by the project owner, and their run
150	artifacts are not publicly available for verification — the published instructions reproduce the harness, not those
151	scores. Windows support is `experimental`.
152	
153	## Quick start
154	
155	If you want to manually configure and start PowerContext, see the [manual startup guide](docs/en/docs/get-started/manual-start.md).
156	
157	PowerContext keeps durable project context outside any single chat. A first run follows this path:
158	
159	```text
160	Agent input → Source evidence → Memory / Topic Memory → PreparedContext → next session or Agent
161	```
162	
163	PowerContext 1.4.1 includes an interactive setup wizard. You need Python 3.11+, Git, and
164	[uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/getting-started/installation/); uv can provision Python when needed. To connect an Agent,
165	also install its CLI. In the wizard, choose Basic memory for explicit saves and recall without model APIs; Full memory
166	requires separate Generation and Embedding API credentials. For a local Server without model APIs, see
167	[Install and run](docs/en/docs/get-started/install-and-run.md).
168	macOS and Linux are supported; Windows support is `experimental`.
169	
170	Choose the path that matches your goal:
171	
172	| Goal | Choose | What you need |
173	| --- | --- | --- |
174	| Try explicit saves and recall | **Basic memory** | CLI and Server; no model API |
175	| Process Sources automatically and use Topic Memory or semantic retrieval | **Full memory** | Separate Generation and Embedding API credentials; schedules and Scope policies still apply |
176	| Learn the core workflows in Python | The first seven Jupyter tutorials | A repository checkout and `uv`; no model or API key |
177	
178	### 1. Install and run the wizard
179	
180	```bash
181	uv tool install --force "powercontext[cli,server]==1.4.1"
182	mkdir -p powercontext-config && cd powercontext-config
183	powercontext config init --language en --output .env
184	powercontext config validate --env-file .env
185	powercontext server run --env-file .env      # keep this terminal open
186	```
187	
188	The wizard asks about storage, local or remote usage, memory capabilities, Dashboard/access, and Agent connections. SQLite is the simplest first storage choice; choose embedded seekdb only when you need it and can approve its dependency install.
189	
190	The wizard writes one `.env` environment file and `.env.next-steps.md`. These files can contain credentials; do not
191	commit them. Keep Server running; in another terminal, return to `powercontext-config`, reload the generated environment
192	file, and check the connection:
193	
194	```bash
195	set -a
196	. ./.env
197	set +a
198	powercontext ready
199	powercontext capabilities
200	```
201	
202	Continue with `.env.next-steps.md` to create and bind the selected Scopes. Use the real `scope_id` returned by the
203	creation request; a planned `codex-...` title is not an ID. Changing directories does not create isolation; use the
204	same Server and Scope in the Dashboard and Agent. Then install the matching plugins and launch a new Agent session.
205	The [complete walkthrough](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/en/docs/get-started/quickstart/) covers Codex and Claude
206	Code, Dashboard login, SSH forwarding, HTTPS prerequisites, and observable acceptance checks. For example, the matching
207	Codex installation is:
208	
209	```bash
210	powercontext setup codex --ref powercontext-v1.4.1
211	powercontext doctor codex
212	```
213	
214	Before sending the first message in a new Codex session, open `/hooks` and, when prompted, trust the PowerContext
215	hooks. Without an enabled `UserPromptSubmit` hook, automatic prompt capture and recall do not run, even if MCP connects.
216	See [first-session checks and recovery](docs/en/docs/integrations/codex.md#first-session-hook-trust).
217	After the first check below, use the [capability and trigger guide](docs/en/docs/get-started/quickstart.md#5-choose-your-next-workflow)
218	to try all seven Artifact families, Source, and Tag. Basic memory needs no separate Server model API; the Agent still uses its own model.
219	
220	### 2. Verify the first end-to-end result
221	
222	This checks the path that makes PowerContext useful: Source capture → background processing → cross-session recall. In
223	the new Agent session bound to the same Scope, send a concrete decision such as:
224	
225	```text
226	We are designing the Aurora acceptance project. Use uv for Python dependencies and .env for configuration.
227	The first deployment runs on one Linux server, with a Mac connecting through an SSH tunnel.
228	Restate these constraints without changing any code.
229	```
230	
231	If you enabled Dashboard, open the URL printed by the wizard; an empty Dashboard before capture is expected. Then:
232	
233	1. Confirm that the prompt appears as a Source.
234	2. After the configured inspection interval and model processing, confirm that a related Topic Memory appears.
235	3. Send a related update and confirm that the new Source arrives and the topic content or revision history reflects it.
236	4. Start a new Agent session with the same Scope and ask for the Aurora decisions with citations.
237	
238	`doctor codex` checks installation and connectivity only. Source capture does not by itself create Memory or Topic
239	Memory, and processing can wait for configured schedules and model work. In Basic memory mode, explicitly ask the Agent
240	to save a decision, then search for it in a new session; that does not test automatic extraction.
241	
242	### 3. Explore Python first — no API key
243	
244	The tutorials run from a repository checkout and take their dependencies from a `uv` dependency group, so they are not
245	part of the `powercontext` package. From the repository root:
246	
247	```bash
248	uv sync --locked --group notebooks
249	uv run --locked --group notebooks jupyter lab --notebook-dir=examples/jupyter
250	```
251	
252	Start with [01 · Memory across sessions](examples/jupyter/01_memory_across_sessions.ipynb), then work through the
253	[22 Chinese Jupyter tutorials and a complete team workflow](examples/jupyter/README.md) to run Memory, context
254	preparation, Handoff, Experience, Skill, and a real Agent step by step. The first seven tutorials need no model or API
255	key.
256	
257	### Common issues
258	
259	- `doctor codex` verifies Codex installation and connectivity. To verify automatic memory, check that a real prompt
260	  becomes a Source, produces a Topic, evolves after a related prompt, and can be recalled in a new session using the
261	  same Scope.
262	- If Python dependency downloads are slow or fail, configure a package index for
263	  [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/configuration/indexes/) and retry.
264	- Choose **Full memory capabilities** to test automatic Memory and Topic Memory; this requires separate Generation and
265	  Embedding API credentials. An Agent subscription does not provide those Server credentials. Choose **Basic memory** to
266	  save and retrieve memories explicitly without additional model APIs.
267	- If seekdb needs installing, the wizard asks once and installs the dependency in the background.
268	- The optional recall-sufficiency gate records scope-local daily search effort and budget omissions for later policy
269	  evaluation. See the [configuration reference](docs/en/docs/operate/configuration.md) for its recording and failure
270	  behavior.
271	- For a Server on another machine, use HTTPS or follow the
272	  [remote connection guide](docs/en/docs/operate/connect-remote-server.md). Setup recognizes remote HTTP URLs from flags
273	  or environment variables and asks for explicit consent; automated setup uses `--allow-insecure-http`.
274

1	# PowerContext
2	
3	**让上下文跟着工作走 —— 跨越会话、跨 Agent、跨人。**
4	
5	[![PyPI version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/powercontext)](https://pypi.org/project/powercontext/)
6	[![License Apache 2.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-Apache_2.0-blue.svg)](LICENSE)

8	[![Ask DeepWiki](https://img.shields.io/badge/Ask-DeepWiki-147DFF)](https://deepwiki.com/oceanbase/powercontext)
9	
10	*[English](README.md) · [中文](README_CN.md) · [日本語](README_JP.md)*
11	
12	[网站](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/) · [快速开始](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/docs/get-started/quickstart/) · [手动启动](docs/zh/docs/get-started/manual-start.md) · [接入 Agent](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/docs/integrations/) · [评测](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/benchmarks/) · [接口](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/docs/develop/interfaces/)
13	
14	## PowerContext 是什么？
15	
16	**PowerContext 是一个面向 AI Agent 的开源上下文服务。** 它把工作里值得长期保留的部分——已经确认的决定、约束、证据、进展——从某一次对话里搬出来，这样下一个会话、下一个 Agent、或者下一个接手的人，都能从当前进展继续，而不必重读全部记录。
17	
18	可以把 PowerContext 理解为三层，也可以直接使用完整 Server 或把同一套契约嵌入其他应用：
19	
20	| 层 | 负责什么 | 适合什么时候使用 |
21	| --- | --- | --- |
22	| Core protocol | `Source`、`Artifact`、`Trigger` 抽象契约 | 嵌入 PowerContext 或扩展组件 |
23	| Builtin runtime | Memory、Handoff、Experience、Skill 服务，以及调度、推理和受支持的存储后端 | 运行标准本地 Server |
24	| Server 与 integrations | HTTP/MCP、Python Client、CLI、Dashboard 和 Agent 插件 | 接入 Agent 或应用 |
25	
26	工作很少会由开始它的人或 Agent 独自完成。你把任务交给 Agent，Agent 推进一部分，之后可能由你或其他人接手。推理过程和当前状态却常常留在那段对话里。PowerContext 把它们从对话里移出来，变成可以查看、带版本号的 Artifact：
27	
28	![你和 Agent 交接工作，并基于已存储的上下文继续推进](docs/assets/readme-workflow.svg)
29	
30	每一项内容都属于一个 **Scope**——一条独立的 Source 日志、一套 Memory 生命周期、一个评审收件箱和一份 Handoff 历史。Scope ID 只用于选取数据，它不证明用户身份、不授予工具访问权限，也不授权执行。
31	
32	```text
33	Scope
34	├── Source journal    # 证据：输入、文档、工作契约、任务结果
35	├── Memory            # atomic-memory/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION —— 决定、约束、下一步
36	├── Review Inbox      # 等待评审的 Experience 与 Skill 候选
37	├── Handoff history   # handoff/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION —— 跨会话的工作接续
38	└── Topic Memory      # 由符合条件的 Source 后台提取
39	```
40	
41	*下面两张 Dashboard 截图使用的是合成演示记录。*
42	
43	### Memory —— 活得比会话更久的决定与约束
44	
45	Memory 是一个带版本的 Artifact 家族，存放可以复用的决定、约束、事实、状态和下一步。条目状态为 `active`、
46	`forgotten`、`merged` 或 `retired`。通过 `POST /v1/atomic-memory/lifecycle` 遗忘一条，会把它移出主动召回，但内容
47	和历史仍然可用、可恢复，且不会推进内容 revision。显式写入 Memory 完全不需要模型；从已捕获的 Source 中提取，
48	才需要配置生成流水线。
49	
50	> "别重构老的 auth 模块 —— 移动端还在用。"
51	> 这么贵的上下文，不应该依赖有人每个会话都重讲一遍。
52	
53	![Dashboard 展示一个 Scope 中活跃的决定和约束，以及对应的 Artifact 与 Source 引用](docs/assets/readme-dashboard-memories-zh.png)
54	
55	### Handoff —— 当前状态，不是整段对话记录
56	
57	Handoff 用一个四步循环记录工作接续：
58	
59	```text
60	工作契约 → Prepared Handoff → 确认回执 → 任务结果
61	```
62	
63	`handoff_current_work` 抓取一个已经检查过的边界，返回一份临时的 Prepared Handoff。只有在你希望留下里程碑时，提交它才会生成持久的 Revision。接收方解析这份 Handoff 并记录确认回执；任务结果则作为 Source 证据保存最终状态与检查项。[Handoff Report](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/docs/workflows/use-handoff-report/) 会把每个选定 Scope 中最新的一份 Handoff Revision 投影出来供查看与导出——只读，且从不改写 Memory。
64	
65	![Dashboard 展示一份已提交的 Handoff Revision，其中包含已记录的进展、下一步和关联的 Source 证据](docs/assets/readme-dashboard-handoff-zh.png)
66	
67	### Experience —— 有用的做法，评审后再复用
68	
69	Experience 记录一个场景、一次动作、观察到的结果，以及可复用的经验教训。它以 `pending` 候选的身份进入 Scope 内的评审收件箱。评审写入使用 `expected_version`，因此在别的写入方改动了提案之后，决策不会静默生效。通过会写入一个不可变的 Revision；驳回则记录原因。两者都是终态，并且只有已通过的当前版本参与召回——待审、被驳回和历史的 Revision 都会被排除。
70	
71	### Skill —— 经过评审的流程，需要显式导出
72	
73	一个受管的 Skill 包含名称、发现描述、指令、校验项和血缘。评审通过**不等于**安装或执行：必须先显式导出某一个明确的已通过 Revision，Agent 宿主才能发现对应的宿主内副本。
74	
75	[运行指标](docs/zh/docs/operate/observability.md)提供依赖状态观察、处理进度新鲜度和执行尝试延迟；独立后台部署也可使用受保护的专用指标端点。
76	
77	## 为什么选 PowerContext？
78	
79	- **上下文活在对话之外。** Memory、Handoff、Experience、Skill 都是服务端 Artifact，不是某个 Agent 家目录里的文件。从 Codex 换到 Claude Code，或者把工作交给同事，工作本身跟着你走。
80	- **一切都带版本、可追溯。** 每个 Artifact 都以 `家族/ARTIFACT_ID@REVISION` 寻址。通过替换产生的后续 Revision 不会改变 Artifact ID，读取一个精确的引用会返回那一份历史快照，即使家族主版本已经前进。
81	- **生成、评审、执行是三个分开的步骤。** 模型可以提出内容，评审通过某个 Revision，导出生成宿主内副本。这三步都不会授予 Agent 执行指令的权限。各个入口目前各自暴露了什么，见[接口说明](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/docs/develop/interfaces/)。
82	- **召回是有边界的，不是整包倾倒。** `PreparedContext` 会按请求选择活跃的 Memory 和已通过的 Experience，并可选加入 Profile 快照和 Topic Memory，再套用一个共享的字节预算，返回 `ready` 内容或 `empty`。召回的内容是历史，不是指令权威：接收方仍然要遵循当前指令，并检查工作区的实时状态。
83	- **复用之前先评审。** Experience 和 Skill 都要经过一次评审决策，无论通过还是驳回都是终态；批准会写入不可变 Revision。启用权限控制后，批准需要 `scope.review`。有审核权限的 Agent 仍可批准自己的 Candidate；需要由人决定时，应使用不同身份和权限[分离 Agent 提交与审核](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/docs/workflows/review-candidates/#分离-agent-提交与审核)。
84	- **一个 Server，同一份契约的多个视图。** HTTP 是完整的远端应用契约。Python Client 提供带类型的访问，MCP 是面向 Agent 的精选投影，Core 协议则允许应用自行组装 Source、Artifact 和 Trigger 实现。
85	
86	### 横向对比
87	
88	以下按常见的单 Agent 或宿主本地部署方式比较；具体能力取决于各系统的部署与集成方式。
89	
90	| | 手动翻聊天记录 | 对聊天记录做 RAG | 宿主本地记忆文件（如 CLAUDE.md） | PowerContext |
91	|---|---|---|---|---|
92	| 跨会话留存 | 手动 | 是 | 是 | 是 |
93	| 换 Agent 后仍可用 | 否 | 否 | 否 | 是 |
94	| 把工作交给另一个人 | 手动 | 手动 | 手动 | Handoff，一等公民 |
95	| 可复用的流程 | 无 | 无 | 仅自然语言描述 | Experience / Skill |
96	| 保留历史版本 | 否 | 取决于索引 | 仅通过 git | 不可变 Revision |
97	| 复用前经过评审 | 不适用 | 否 | 否 | 评审收件箱 |
98	| 单轮召回有预算约束 | 否 | 部分 | 否 | `PreparedContext` 字节预算 |
99	| 可以在 Agent 之外查看 | 否 | 否 | 是 | 是 |
100	
101	## 效果怎么样
102	
103	![PowerContext 在 LoCoMo 和 SWE-bench Pro 上的紧凑对比图](docs/assets/readme-benchmark-summary.svg)
104	
105	- **长对话记忆（LoCoMo；类别 1–4 的 1,540 个计分问题）：准确率 90.78%**，PowerMem 为 87.79%，全量上下文
106	  重放为 52.9% —— 相对后者 **+37.88 个百分点**。
107	- **编码任务（SWE-bench Pro；731 个公开 v2 任务的配对项目运行，非官方提交）：开启 PowerContext 后任务解决率
108	  86.73%，关闭时 82.35%** —— **+4.38 个百分点**，多解出 32 题。
109	- **召回质量（LoCoMo Plus）**：原生 PowerContext 对比检索后用 Jev 做候选筛选。每组模型同时用于 Memory 抽取与回答，向量模型为 `qwen3.7-text-embedding`（1024 维）：
110	
111	  | 模型 | PowerContext | PowerContext + Jev | 差值 |
112	  |---|---|---|---|
113	  | GPT-4o-mini | 40.855% | 49.78% | +8.925 个百分点 |
114	  | Qwen3.7-plus | 60.37% | 68.947% | +8.577 个百分点 |
115	  | GPT-4o | 65.082% | 69.28% | +4.198 个百分点 |
116	
117	评测方法、完整结果和适用边界：[官网评测页](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/benchmarks/)。有两点我们宁愿写明而不是藏起来：上面这六组 LoCoMo Plus 分数由项目方提供，运行产物尚未公开供复核——公开的是评测工具的复现步骤，不代表这些分数本身已被公开验证。Windows 支持为 `experimental`。
118	
119	## 快速开始
120	
121	如果希望自己一步一步配置并启动 PowerContext，请查看[手动启动文档](docs/zh/docs/get-started/manual-start.md)。
122	
123	PowerContext 把可长期复用的项目上下文放在单次对话之外。一次完整体验可以概括为：
124	
125	```text
126	Agent 输入 → Source 证据 → Memory / Topic Memory → PreparedContext → 新会话或新 Agent
127	```
128	
129	PowerContext 1.4.1 包含交互式配置向导。需要 Python 3.11+、Git 和
130	[uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/getting-started/installation/)；需要时 uv 可以按需提供 Python。
131	要接入 Agent，还需要安装对应的 CLI。向导中选择“基础记忆”即可在没有模型 API 的情况下显式保存与召回；
132	选择“完整记忆能力”则需要独立的 Generation、Embedding API 凭据。只想启动不依赖模型 API 的本地 Server 时，
133	请参考[安装与运行](docs/zh/docs/get-started/install-and-run.md)。
134	支持 macOS 和 Linux；Windows 支持为 `experimental`。
135	
136	请选择与你目标匹配的路径：
137	
138	| 目标 | 选择 | 需要 |
139	| --- | --- | --- |
140	| 体验显式保存与召回 | **基础记忆** | CLI 和 Server；无需模型 API |
141	| 自动处理 Source，使用 Topic Memory 或语义检索 | **完整记忆能力** | 独立的 Generation、Embedding API 凭据；仍受调度和 Scope 策略影响 |
142	| 用 Python 学习核心工作流 | 前七篇 Jupyter 教程 | 仓库 checkout 和 `uv`；不需要模型或 API Key |
143	
144	### 1. 安装并运行配置向导
145	
146	```bash
147	uv tool install --force "powercontext[cli,server]==1.4.1"
148	mkdir -p powercontext-config && cd powercontext-config
149	powercontext config init --language zh --output .env
150	powercontext config validate --env-file .env
151	powercontext server run --env-file .env      # 保持这个终端不关
152	```
153	
154	向导会询问存储、本机或远程使用场景、记忆能力、Dashboard/访问控制和 Agent 连接。首次体验可以使用 SQLite；只有确实需要时再选择嵌入式 seekdb，并同意依赖安装。
155	
156	向导生成一个 `.env` 环境文件和 `.env.next-steps.md`。这些文件可能包含凭据，请勿提交到 Git。保持 Server 运行，在另一个终端回到 `powercontext-config` 目录，重新加载生成的环境文件并检查服务：
157	
158	```bash
159	set -a
160	. ./.env
161	set +a
162	powercontext ready
163	powercontext capabilities
164	```
165	
166	接着按 `.env.next-steps.md` 创建并绑定 Scope。使用创建请求返回的真实 `scope_id`；向导规划的 `codex-...` 标题不是 ID。
167	切换目录不会创建隔离；Dashboard 和 Agent 要使用同一个 Server 和 Scope。然后安装相同版本的插件，再打开新 Agent 会话。
168	[完整安装流程](https://powercontext.oceanbase.io/zh/docs/get-started/quickstart/)包含 Codex、Claude Code、Dashboard 登录、SSH 隧道、HTTPS 前提及逐项验收。例如，匹配本版本的 Codex 安装命令是：
169	
170	```bash
171	powercontext setup codex --ref powercontext-v1.4.1
172	powercontext doctor codex
173	```
174	
175	在新 Codex 会话发送第一条消息前，打开 `/hooks` 检查 PowerContext Hook，并在收到提示时授予信任。
176	`UserPromptSubmit` Hook 未启用时，即使 MCP 连通，也不会自动采集输入或召回上下文。
177	检查与恢复步骤见[首次会话 Hook 信任](docs/zh/docs/integrations/codex.md#首次会话-hook-信任)。
178	完成下面的首次验收后，可按[能力与触发指南](docs/zh/docs/get-started/quickstart.md#5-选择下一项工作流)
179	体验七类 Artifact、Source 和 Tag。基础记忆无需独立的 Server 模型 API；Agent 仍使用自己的模型。
180	
181	### 2. 验收第一条端到端结果
182	
183	这一步检查 PowerContext 真正有用的链路：Source 采集 → 后台处理 → 跨会话召回。
184	在绑定了同一 Scope 的新 Agent 会话中，发送一条具体决定，例如：
185	
186	```text
187	我们正在设计 Aurora 验收项目。决定用 uv 管理 Python 依赖，配置文件使用 .env。
188	第一阶段只在一台 Linux 服务器运行，Mac 通过 SSH 隧道访问。请复述这些约束，暂时不要修改代码。
189	```
190	
191	如果开启了 Dashboard，打开向导打印的地址；采集前页面为空是正常现象。然后：
192	
193	1. 确认这条输入出现在 Source 中。
194	2. 等待配置的检查周期和模型处理，确认出现相关 Topic Memory。
195	3. 发送一条相关更新，确认新的 Source 入库，且主题内容或修订记录反映了更新。
196	4. 使用同一 Scope 开启新的 Agent 会话，要求它带引用找回 Aurora 的决定。
197	
198	`doctor codex` 只检查安装与连接。Source 采集本身不会创建 Memory 或 Topic Memory，后台处理还要等待配置的调度和模型运行。
199	基础记忆模式下，可以显式要求 Agent 保存一条决定，再在新会话中搜索；这不能验收自动提取。
200	
201	### 3. 先用 Python 体验
202	
203	教程需要在仓库 checkout 内运行，依赖由 `uv` 的依赖组提供，不在 `powercontext` 包内。在仓库根目录执行：
204	
205	```bash
206	uv sync --locked --group notebooks
207	uv run --locked --group notebooks jupyter lab --notebook-dir=examples/jupyter
208	```
209	
210	先从 [01 · 让新会话用上项目约定](examples/jupyter/01_memory_across_sessions.ipynb)开始，再通过
211	[22 篇中文 Jupyter 教程与完整团队工作流](examples/jupyter/README.md)亲手运行 Memory、上下文、交接、Experience、Skill
212	和真实 Agent。前七篇不需要模型或 API Key。
213	
214	### 常见问题
215	
216	- `doctor codex` 只验证 Codex 集成的安装和连接，不代表自动记忆流程已通过。要验收自动记忆，需要确认真实输入进入 Source、产生 Topic、相关输入推动主题演进，并能在使用同一 Scope 的新会话中召回。
217	- Python 依赖下载缓慢或失败时，可先为 [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/configuration/indexes/) 配置包索引（镜像源）再重试。
218	- 要体验自动 Memory 提取和 Topic Memory，请选择**完整记忆能力**并准备独立的 Generation、Embedding API 凭据；Agent 官方订阅不会自动给 PowerContext Server 提供这些凭据。选择**基础记忆**则可显式保存与召回，无需额外模型 API。
219	- 选择 seekdb 且缺少依赖时，会在确认后后台增量安装。
220	- 可选的召回充分性门控会按 Scope 记录每日搜索工作量和字节预算遗漏，供后续策略评估使用。记录范围与失败行为见[配置说明](docs/zh/docs/operate/configuration.md)。
221	- Server 部署在另一台机器上时，请使用 HTTPS 或参考[远程连接说明](docs/zh/docs/operate/connect-remote-server.md)。配置向导会从参数或环境变量识别远端 HTTP 地址并要求显式确认；自动化场景使用 `--allow-insecure-http`。
222

5	
6	# Choose an interface
7	
8	All remote interfaces operate on the same Server and persistent Artifact storage.
9	
10	| Interface | Intended use | Start here |
11	| --- | --- | --- |
12	| Codex plugin | Cross-session recall and explicit Memory maintenance in Codex | [Configure Codex](../integrations/codex.md) |
13	| Claude Code plugin | Cross-session recall and Handoff in Claude Code | [Configure Claude Code](../integrations/claude-code.md) |
14	| DeepSeek Harness plugin | Recall and explicit Memory maintenance in DeepSeek Harness | [Configure DeepSeek Harness](../integrations/dsh.md) |
15	| Hermes integration | Recall, Memory, and Handoff tools in Hermes | [Configure Hermes](../integrations/hermes.md) |
16	| OpenClaw plugin | Bounded recall and durable Memory tools in OpenClaw | [Configure OpenClaw](../integrations/openclaw.md) |
17	| OpenCode plugin | Recall and Memory maintenance in OpenCode | [Configure OpenCode](../integrations/opencode.md) |
18	| Pi package | Recall, native Memory/Handoff tools, and skills in Pi | [Configure Pi](../integrations/pi.md) |
19	| WorkBuddy integration | Prompt recall, MCP tools, and Handoff in WorkBuddy | [Configure WorkBuddy](../integrations/workbuddy.md) |
20	| Pydantic AI adapter | Preview API; no supported standalone install yet | [Adapter status](../integrations/pydantic-ai.md) |
21	| LangChain middleware | Bounded recall and completed-turn Source capture in `create_agent` | [Install from source](../integrations/langchain.md) |
22	| LangGraph adapter | Memory tools and bounded recall inside a LangGraph graph | [Install from source](../integrations/langgraph.md) |
23	| CLI | Setup, diagnostics, Server control, and human Candidate review | [Install and run](../get-started/install-and-run.md) |
24	| Python Client SDK | Typed asynchronous calls to a running Server | [Install the client role](../get-started/install-and-run.md#install-a-python-role) |
25	| Core SDK | In-process Source, Artifact, Trigger, and composition contracts | [Python API reference](/en/modules/) |
26	| HTTP | Service integration from any language | [HTTP API](http-api.md) |
27	| MCP | Curated Agent tools for Source capture, Memory, work continuity, reports, and Candidate Review | Enabled by the Server at `/mcp` |
28	
29	## Codex plugin
30	
31	The powercontext-project-context skill tells Codex when to search, remember, revise, retire, delegate, hand off, acknowledge, or
32	record an outcome. The prompt hook recalls relevant entries and captures user input as Source evidence. MCP tools
33	perform explicit operations. The plugin never starts or embeds the Server.
34	
35	## Work continuity
36	
37	HTTP, the Python Client, and MCP expose Work Contract creation, Handoff preparation and continuation, acknowledgement,
38	and Task Outcome recording. A Prepared Handoff is temporary; `commit_handoff` creates a durable Revision. An
39	acknowledgement can select a prepared or exact Handoff, but `handoff_receipt_ref` accepts only an accepted exact Receipt
40	for a committed Revision. Claims and checks can be `declared` or `verified` with exact same-scope citations. These
41	records never grant identity, tool, or execution authority.
42	
43	```text
44	create_work_contract
45	  -> work
46	  -> handoff_current_work
47	  -> continue_handoff + acknowledge_handoff
48	  -> record_task_outcome
49	```
50	
51	`create_work_contract` records the objective, scope, completion criteria, authority notes, and consequential open
52	questions for newly delegated work. `handoff_current_work` captures caller-inspected state and returns a temporary
53	Prepared Handoff; it does not publish a milestone. Call `commit_handoff` separately when the user wants a durable
54	milestone.
55	
56	The receiver calls `continue_handoff` with a prepared, exact, or latest selection. When starting from latest, the
57	returned exact Revision is shown and inspected before acknowledgement. `acknowledge_handoff` accepts prepared or exact,
58	never latest. It refuses acceptance when any Handoff evidence is unavailable or when live-state, capability, and
59	authorization are not all `confirmed`. A receiver can instead record `needs_clarification` or `declined`. The receipt
60	and its three confirmations are untrusted observations; they grant no identity, tool, or execution authority.
61	
62	`record_task_outcome` preserves `succeeded`, `partial`, `blocked`, `failed`, `cancelled`, or `unknown` and exact check
63	states. To cover a committed Handoff result, `handoff_receipt_ref` identifies the active accepted exact Receipt; an
64	unlinked Outcome in the same scope does not cover it. The operation stores a `task-outcome` Source that existing
65	Experience incubation can inspect, but does not generate or approve an Experience by itself. Integrations call it only
66	at a real completion or interruption boundary, not solely because a prompt, Stop event, or Session ended.
67	
68	Claims and checks are either `declared` with no evidence or `verified` with exact same-scope citations. A readable
69	citation proves identity and availability, not freshness. Current instructions, live workspace state, capabilities,
70	and authorization still take precedence over all Work and Handoff records.
71	
72	For the complete Codex transfer and acknowledgement workflow, see
73	[Hand off work in Codex](../workflows/handoff-with-codex.md).
74	
75	Handoff Report is a read-only projection over a Scope selection. `all` includes every Scope, `exact` includes only the
76	listed Scope IDs, and `subtree` includes an organization root and all descendants. Each included Scope contributes its
77	latest exact Handoff address or an explicit `no_handoff` result; Parent does not imply Context visibility. Codex fixes
78	ordinary Agent report reads to the current Session Scope. Explicit HTTP clients may request broader selections.
79	See [Use Handoff Report](../workflows/use-handoff-report.md) for the report API.
80	
81	## DeepSeek Harness plugin
82	
83	The powercontext-project-context skill tells DeepSeek Harness when to search, remember, revise, or retire Memory. Before each model
84	step the plugin recalls relevant entries and captures user input as Source evidence. Named `pc_*` tools perform explicit
85	HTTP operations. The plugin never starts or embeds the Server.
86	
87	## Pydantic AI adapter
88	
89	The repository contains a preview Pydantic AI adapter with three Memory tools and automatic bounded
90	`PreparedContext`. It is not currently available as a supported standalone package. Optional capture stores
91	redacted, bounded visible model and completed tool events, performs checkpoint Flush, and flushes remaining Sources
92	after the run. MCP needs no adapter package but does not provide automatic context preparation, capture, or Flush.
93	See [Pydantic AI adapter preview](../integrations/pydantic-ai.md).
94	
95	## LangGraph adapter
96	
97	`powercontext-langgraph` connects a LangGraph graph to a running Server through the public Python Client. It supplies
98	three components: `powercontext_tools()` returns `BaseTool` instances for model-initiated Memory read and write;
99	`PowerContextRecall` is a node or `pre_model_hook` that prepends one bounded `PreparedContext` as a system message
100	labelled untrusted historical evidence; and `PowerContextScope` is a dataclass for the graph `context_schema` that
101	carries the scope and per-run connection overrides. The recall node and tools read the active scope from the LangGraph
102	runtime and otherwise fall back to `POWERCONTEXT_LANGGRAPH_*` environment settings.
103	
104	Scope resolution sends an explicit `scope_id`, when configured, to the Server for validation and otherwise uses the
105	Server default Scope. The adapter does not derive Scope IDs from Git or process paths. `TOKEN` is a bare token that the
106	Client composes into `Authorization: Bearer`, unlike the `POWERCONTEXT_*_AUTHORIZATION` header used by the Codex,
107	Claude Code, and DeepSeek Harness plugins. Recall and the tools fail open: on Server unavailability the graph still
108	reaches its end and the tools return a short unavailable string. The adapter covers Memory read and write and bounded
109	recall only; automatic capture, checkpointing, and Handoff are out of scope. The adapter deliberately does not
110	implement `BaseStore`, whose get, upsert-by-key, and delete operations the Memory model does not provide. It never
111	starts or embeds the Server.
112	
113	## LangChain middleware
114	
115	`PowerContextMiddleware` uses LangChain's `AgentMiddleware` API. It injects one bounded PreparedContext into each
116	current model request without changing agent state. Automatic capture is disabled by default; pass `auto_capture=True`
117	to capture the latest user message and final plain-text or structured answer as Content Source evidence after a
118	successful run. Source-to-Memory activation remains a Server responsibility. Recall and capture fail open, and neither
119	path starts or embeds the Server. Its source is packaged as `powercontext-langchain`, but it is not currently
120	published on PyPI. The LangGraph adapter remains a separate node-and-tool integration.
121	
122	## Pi package
123	
124	The native Pi package supplies the `powercontext-project-context` skill, named `pc_*` Memory and Handoff tools, and `/pc`
125	diagnostics. Before each normal agent start, it requests one strict, bounded PreparedContext value and independently
126	captures an eligible user prompt as Source evidence. It does not synchronize Pi transcripts. Recall, capture, and
127	boundary flushing fail open; explicit durable writes require interactive confirmation.
128	
129	## CLI
130	
131	Set `POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID` to an existing ID returned by `create_scope` before running scoped content commands.
132	
133	```text
134	powercontext setup [agents...]
135	powercontext setup codex dsh
136	powercontext config agent codex dsh --server-url https://memory.example
137	powercontext config init --output .env
138	powercontext config show --env-file .env
139	powercontext config validate --env-file .env
140	powercontext doctor
141	powercontext doctor codex dsh
142	powercontext server run
143	powercontext server run --env-file .env
144	powercontext ready
145	powercontext capabilities
146	powercontext experience generate --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID" --source-ref content/SOURCE_ID
147	powercontext experience list --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID"
148	powercontext experience show --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID" --revision 1 EXPERIENCE_ID
149	powercontext skill generate --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID" --origin experience \
150	  --artifact-ref experience/EXPERIENCE_ID@REVISION
151	powercontext skill show --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID" --revision 1 SKILL_ID
152	powercontext skill export --target codex --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID" --revision 1 \
153	  --destination .agents/skills/example-skill SKILL_ID
154	powercontext external-skill scan --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID"
155	powercontext external-skill list --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID"
156	powercontext external-skill resolve --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID" --fingerprint SHA256 EXTERNAL_SKILL_ID
157	powercontext external-skill import --scope-id "$POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID" --fingerprint SHA256 \
158	  --mode import EXTERNAL_SKILL_ID
159	```
160	
161	All content commands call the configured Server. The optional `server` role adds `powercontext server run`; it does
162	not create a second content profile inside the CLI.
163	
164	The `config` group creates, redacts, and validates an explicit environment file. `server run` discovers `.env` in the
165	current directory; pass `--env-file` to select another file or `--no-env-file` to disable discovery. See
166	[Configuration](../operate/configuration.md) for precedence and credential-handling rules.
167	
168	Use `codex`, `claude-code`, `dsh`, `hermes`, `kimi-code`, `openclaw`, `opencode`, `pi`, `workbuddy`, or `zcode` for agents.
169	`powercontext setup` offers interactive selection; explicit agent names support non-interactive and multi-agent setup.
170	Setup installs, configures, activates where supported, and runs doctor. Failures remain isolated per agent; completed installation is reported even when diagnostics fail.
171	
172	`powercontext config agent [agents...]` updates installed connections without reinstalling or enabling plugins.
173	`powercontext doctor [agents...]` checks the selected agents, or saved connections when none are selected.
174	With no saved connections, doctor checks the package and Server. Selected agents with a missing required CLI fail their own checks.
175	Doctor preserves host-specific checks, including OpenCode's separate `plugin` and `skill` results. DSH checks its native registration; Pi checks its registered package.
176	
177	The unified HTTP review routes are `/v1/candidates/list`, `get`, `history`, `revise`, `approve`, and `reject` (all POST).
178	`candidate_kind=artifact|tag` distinguishes proposals. Old Artifact Candidate routes are removed; callers must upgrade together.
179	Artifact approval returns a real revision; Tag approval returns committed tags and ETag without creating a content revision.
180	
181	Review decisions remain available through MCP. With access control enforced, Server authorization requires
182	`scope.review`; it does not prohibit an authorized proposer from approving its own Candidate. MCP tool annotations let
183	hosts apply confirmation policies but do not require human confirmation. See
184	[Review authority](../workflows/review-candidates.md#review-authority) for access modes and reviewer separation.
185	
186	The `candidate` command group exposes the human Review Inbox. See [Review Candidates](../workflows/review-candidates.md)
187	for the ordered workflow to list, inspect, revise, approve, or reject Candidates.
188	
189	Generation and revision commands accept repeatable `--source-ref TYPE/ID` and
190	`--artifact-ref FAMILY/ID@REVISION` options instead of serialized request files. `--target FAMILY/ID@REVISION`
191	automatically includes the target in Artifact evidence. Managed Skill revision accepts exactly one of inline
192	`--instructions` or `--instructions-file`, and `--validation` can be repeated.
193	
194	## Python Client SDK
195	
196	`PowerContextClient` is the typed asynchronous HTTP client for a Server-owned deployment. Its request and response
197	models are exported from `powercontext.http`. Memory mutation `records` contain real ArtifactRefs, content, and current
198	state. Content edits use the content ETag; lifecycle operations use the required content and state versions. Exact
199	history is read as Atomic Memory Artifact revisions. See the [HTTP API lifecycle tutorial](api-quickstart.md) for a runnable client
200	flow and [Atomic Memory](../workflows/atomic-memory.md) for lifecycle operations.
201	
202	The Client also exposes `generate_experience`, `propose_experience`, `get_experience`, `generate_skill`,
203	`propose_skill`, `get_skill`, `scan_external_skills`, `list_external_skills`, `resolve_external_skill`,
204	`import_external_skill`, and the Candidate Review methods. Review writes require `expected_version`. Approval returns
205	the exact Experience or managed Skill `result_artifact`; pending and rejected Candidates are not Artifact revisions.
206	
207	`generate_experience` and `generate_skill` accept caller-selected exact Source and Artifact references. They return
208	either one pending Candidate or an explicit `no_op`. A replacement includes its exact target in `artifact_refs` and
209	sets `target`. Managed Skill generation also declares its provenance shape:
210	
211	- `experience`: at least one approved Experience reference, with optional exact Sources;
212	- `source`: only exact Source references, including official or human-authored material;
213	- `usage`: the exact target Skill plus bounded usage Sources.
214	
215	These generation operations require `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_INFERENCE_GENERATION_MODEL`. The lower-level `propose_*`
216	operations remain available for a human or integration that already has complete typed content and exact evidence.
217	Both paths return pending content; approval is a separate operation. After Review approval, deterministic
218	`searchable_text` is stored on the existing generic Artifact head and enters the backend's rebuildable FTS index, making the Experience eligible for
219	`PreparedContext` recall in the same scope. Pending and rejected Candidates, all managed Skills, and historical
220	Experience revisions remain excluded.
221	
222	For the relationship between evidence, Candidate versions, approved Revisions, recall, and export, see
223	[Experience and Skill lifecycle](../workflows/experience-and-skill-lifecycle.md).
224	
225	## Scheduled Atomic Memory processing
226	
227	The built-in Source-window schedule compares journal positions with its own persisted consumption cursor before
228	activating a Scope. Fully consumed journals skip scoped processing, while buffered windows remain eligible on later
229	ticks without requiring another captured Source. Discovery is advisory: authorization and cursor/window checks
230	still run in the selected Scope's processing operation, and captures committed after discovery remain visible to
231	subsequent ticks.
232	
233	This selection applies to the built-in Source-window policy. A directly assembled Runtime with a custom Scope
234	selector retains that selector, and Experience incubation uses its independent cursor and schedule.
235	
236	## Scheduled Experience incubation
237	
238	An integration can capture a completed task as a Content Source with metadata `"kind": "task-outcome"`. When the
239	Experience schedule is configured, APScheduler scans bounded Source windows and asks the configured schema-bound
240	pipeline for reusable situation, action, outcome, and lesson proposals. Each proposal cites exact Sources and enters
241	the Review Inbox as a pending Experience Candidate.
242	
243	Experience incubation has its own persisted Source cursor, independent from Memory extraction. Candidate writes and
244	cursor advancement commit together; a generation or write failure leaves the window available for retry. Ordinary
245	prompt Sources are not Task Outcomes and are ignored by this job.
246	
247	Scheduling stops at the review boundary. It never approves an Experience, includes pending content in
248	PreparedContext, derives a managed Skill, exports a Skill for an Agent target, or executes instructions. Skill authoring and
249	export remain explicit steps after the supporting Experience is approved.
250	Setup and verification steps are in
251	[Create and review an Experience](../workflows/create-and-review-experience.md).
252	
253	## Managed Skill export to Agent targets
254	
255	A configured generator can produce complete managed Skill content through `generate_skill`; a human or integration
256	can submit already-complete typed content through `propose_skill`. The proposal contains a name, discovery
257	description, instructions, validation checks, and exact Source or Artifact lineage. It remains a Candidate until a
258	reviewer approves the exact Candidate version.
259	
260	Approval creates an immutable Skill Revision. It does not install the Skill or grant execution authority. To make one
261	approved Revision available to Codex or Claude Code, export it explicitly into a configured repository, user, or plugin
262	Skill target. The projection writes `SKILL.md` and `powercontext.json`; the manifest records the Agent kind, exact
263	Artifact reference and rendered-content hash. It refuses to replace an existing destination, so updates require an
264	intentional new export rather than a silent overwrite.
265	
266	Codex can discover a repository-local export under `.agents/skills/<name>/SKILL.md`. The Artifact Revision remains
267	the content authority; Claude Code uses `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` for the equivalent project target. Both
268	directories are host-local projections that can be rebuilt from the same exact Revision.
269	See [Create and export a managed Skill](../workflows/create-and-export-skill.md) for the procedure.
270	
271	## External Agent-native Skills
272	
273	External Skills remain authoritative in their original local packages. With explicitly configured Agent targets, the
274	Server can scan a scope-local, rebuildable Registry and report name, description, provider, Agent kind, host,
275	installation scope, locator, and whole-package fingerprint. Exact resolve succeeds only when the same package remains
276	readable on the configured host and its fingerprint still matches. It never installs a package or falls back to a
277	different version.
278	
279	Discovery does not enter Review. An explicit `import_external_skill` request with the exact identity and fingerprint
280	captures the validated whole package and records Source evidence for that snapshot. With `mode=import`, the Runtime
281	proposes the captured package directly, preserving its file paths and bytes, including scripts and resources, without
282	calling a generation model. With `mode=fork`, a configured generator uses the snapshot as evidence for a new managed
283	Skill proposal and may return `no_op` without a Candidate. Capturing the original package does not guarantee that the
284	generated proposal retains its scripts or resources.
285	
286	When either mode returns a Candidate, it remains pending until Review approval creates a new managed Skill identity.
287	Neither mode edits the original external package. Later changes that alter the package fingerprint make the old
288	fingerprint unavailable for resolution or import; they do not replace an already approved managed Revision.
289	
290	## Authority and gates
291	
292	| Surface | Content authority | Model gate | Review gate | Current availability |
293	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
294	| External Agent-native Skill | Original package | No for scan/list/resolve/import; yes for fork | No for discovery; yes for import/fork Candidates | Host-local Registry and exact resolve |
295	| Experience | Exact approved Artifact Revision | Yes for generate/evolve; no for typed `propose` | Yes | Exact read and approved-head FTS recall in PreparedContext |
296	| Managed Skill | Exact approved Artifact Revision | Yes for generate/evolve/fork; no for import or typed `propose` | Yes | Exact read and explicit Agent projection |
297	| Agent projection | Its source managed Skill Revision | No | No additional review | Rebuildable Codex or Claude Code host-local copy |
298	
299	## Core SDK
300	
301	The base `powercontext` package exports Python protocols and models for applications that own their composition root.
302	It does not select storage, scheduling, transport, or inference on the application's behalf. Use `builtin` when you
303	want the supplied SQLite or OceanBase-backed implementation in the same process.
304	
305	## HTTP and MCP
306	
307	See [HTTP API](http-api.md) for authentication, curl examples, operation groups, errors, and the complete OpenAPI
308	contract. The Server publishes a Scalar API reference at `/docs`, its OpenAPI document at `/openapi.json`, readiness at
309	`/health/ready`, capabilities at `/v1/capabilities`, and Streamable HTTP MCP at `/mcp` by default. The Scalar reference
310	remains public when bearer authentication is enabled, but the operations it describes retain their normal authentication
311	requirements. HTTP is the complete application contract. MCP is a curated Agent-facing projection of Source capture,
312	Memory maintenance, work continuity, scope Handoff Report lookup, and Candidate Review. The six Candidate Review
313	operations use the same validation, `expected_version` concurrency checks, and approval transaction over HTTP and MCP.
314	Readiness is `ready` with HTTP 200 when all checks pass, `degraded` with HTTP 200 when only configured inference checks
315	fail, and `not_ready` with HTTP 503 when the Runtime or database fails. Dependency checks use `ready`, `unavailable`,
316	`timeout`, or `misconfigured`; an intentionally unbound Runtime reports `not_ready` for the `runtime` check.
317	Experience and Skill generation, exact reads, external Registry operations, and low-level proposal operations remain
318	HTTP-only.
319	
320	MCP uses stateless Streamable HTTP and does not issue MCP session IDs. When authentication is enabled, each
321	request uses its own verified identity and current group membership. Scopes, Memory, and Handoffs remain
322	persisted in the Runtime. Reconnect MCP clients after upgrading; cross-request SSE stream resumption is
323	unavailable.
324	
325	`POST /v1/context/prepare` and the matching Python Client method expose final ephemeral `PreparedContext` over HTTP;
326	the Runtime recalls active Memory and approved Experience heads, owns their shared selection and total output budget,
327	and intentionally does not project the operation as an MCP tool. The public schema remains
328	`powercontext.prepared-context.v1`; Experience items carry an exact Artifact reference inside the prepared content.
329

1	# 用 Python 一步步体验 PowerContext
2	
3	这套教程从开发订单 CSV 导入器开始：保存项目约定，维护变化的规则，为当前问题准备上下文，
4	交接尚未完成的工作，再把验证过的做法保存为 Experience 和 Skill，最后接入真实 Agent。
5	
6	每篇都有场景说明、可执行代码、输出解读和练习。核心调用直接留在单元格里，
7	可以改一个输入，再运行观察变化。共 22 篇：11 篇基础与 HTTP 教程、10 篇进阶实验，以及 1 篇完整团队工作流。前七篇不需要模型或 API Key。
8	想先看完整过程，可以直接打开 [22 · 从接手到团队复用](22_complete_team_workflow.ipynb)。
9	下文给出课程的接口选择、功能覆盖和成功标准。
10	
11	## 先运行第一篇
12	
13	基础篇需要 Python 3.11+；完整课程（含 OpenDAL）需要 Python 3.12+、`uv` 和这个仓库的完整 checkout。在**仓库根目录的终端**执行：
14	
15	```bash
16	uv sync --locked --group notebooks
17	uv run --locked --group notebooks jupyter lab --notebook-dir=examples/jupyter
18	```
19	
20	在浏览器打开终端显示的 Jupyter 地址，选择 `01_memory_across_sessions.ipynb`，
21	Kernel 选择 **Python 3 (ipykernel)**，用 **Shift+Enter** 逐格运行。
22	安装完成后，也可以在仓库根目录用 `make notebooks` 启动。
23	
24	01–10、12–22 的首个代码单元会启动真实本地 Server，监听随机回环端口，默认使用独立 SQLite 数据库。
25	每篇都会准备自己的 Scope 和材料，不依赖上一篇的运行结果。数据库放在本目录 `.powercontext/` 下，
26	环境初始化不会连接已有业务数据库。第一次安装需要访问软件包源，前七篇在安装后可以离线运行。
27	
28	第一篇运行成功时，你会看到：
29	
30	1. 新项目最初没有可用上下文。
31	2. 保存金额约定后，`prepare_context` 返回含该约定的文本与引用，可直接加入模型消息。
32	3. 新建 Client 连接仍能读到这条知识。
33	4. 重启 Server 后，同一制品的历史版本仍然可读。
34	5. 最后一个单元格关闭本篇 Server。
35	
36	## 使用 OceanBase 运行
37	
38	01–10、12–22 的代码也可以连接 OceanBase MySQL 模式。先准备一个专供本套教程使用的测试数据库，
39	然后在 `examples/jupyter/.env` 或启动 Jupyter 的环境中配置 `POWERCONTEXT_NOTEBOOK_OCEANBASE_URL`。
40	连接格式为 `mysql+aoceanbase://user:encoded-password@host:2881/powercontext_notebooks`，密码中的特殊字符需要 URL 编码。
41	模板中有该配置项；不要把实际连接串写进单元格。
42	
43	```bash
44	make notebooks
45	# 配置真实模型后，执行模型篇及默认篇
46	make notebooks-test ARGS="--with-models"
47	```
48	
49	初始化表格会显示实际后端 `oceanbase`。每次实验的幂等键带有本次运行标识，完整重跑会创建新的 Scope；
50	同一次实验中重复发送相同创建请求仍保持幂等。Server 重启后继续连接同一测试库。
51	不设置该变量时保持 SQLite；辅助代码不会自动继承 `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_DATABASE_URL` 连接业务库。
52	
53	OceanBase 测试数据会保留在专用数据库中，关闭 Kernel 或清理本地目录不会删除它。
54	第 11 篇使用独立 Server，需要将该 Server 的 `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_DATABASE_KIND` 设为 `oceanbase`，
55	并将 `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_DATABASE_URL` 指向专用测试库；HTTP Notebook 的业务请求无需改变。
56	可复制 [OceanBase Server 模板](server-http-oceanbase.env.example) 到本目录 `.powercontext/http-oceanbase.env`，
57	填入连接串，再将 HTTP 启动命令的 `--env-file` 指向这个文件。
58

115	## 这些示例怎样选择接口
116	
117	| 要做的事情 | 优先使用 |
118	| --- | --- |
119	| 保存和搜索记忆 | `remember_memory` 返回 `records`；`search_memory` 的命中记录位于 `hit.memory` |
120	| 修订和遗忘记忆 | `replace_artifact` 携带正文 ETag；`change_atomic_memory_lifecycle` 携带精确引用与状态版本 |
121	| 让本次请求用上已有知识 | `prepare_context`；Agent 可直接接入 `PowerContextMiddleware` |
122	| 保存原始材料或直接提交明确内容 | `create_source`、统一的 `create_artifact` |
123	| 按制品身份查看内容和版本 | `get_artifact`、`list_artifacts`、`get_artifact_revision` |
124	| 审核尚待判断的内容 | proposal / generation → 检查 Candidate → approve / reject |
125	| 独立应用接入与并发更新 | HTTP 专题中的 JSON 请求、Server ETag 与 `If-Match` |
126	| 把外部身份对应到项目 | `set_scope_binding` / `resolve_scope_binding`；再把解析出的 Scope 交给 Middleware |
127	
128	01–04 中的每条记忆都是独立的 `atomic-memory` Artifact，搜索与上下文准备在指定 Scope 内召回 active 记忆。
129	`remember_memory` 创建后的记录使用 `record.artifact` 精确引用正文版本；统一 `create_artifact(family="atomic-memory")`
130	也可以创建同类记忆。正文 revision 与生命周期 state_version 分别变化，历史正文通过精确 Artifact 引用读取。
131	
132	Experience、Skill 和 Handoff 使用统一 API 直接创建；Candidate 是待确认内容的审核流程。
133	Handoff 的主路径是预览再 `commit_handoff`；统一 `create_artifact` 只在内容已经写好时使用。
134	当前 Python Client 未在普通制品读取结果中暴露 ETag；02 用真实 HTTP GET 读取正文 ETag，再交给 Client 执行条件替换。
135	第 11 篇使用普通 HTTP 请求演示条件读取与更新。
136	进阶篇的具体入口和成功证据见上方学习路径。
137	
138	## 本课覆盖与刻意不覆盖
139	
140	主线覆盖 Source、Atomic Memory、PreparedContext、Experience / Skill 审核、工作交接闭环、提取与生成、
141	三种检索和重排、LangChain Middleware、LangGraph Memory 工具以及独立 HTTP 制品契约。
142	进阶篇继续覆盖团队共享和权限、OpenDAL、后台孵化、Skill 使用反馈、外部完整包、托管发布与 Receiver、
143	MCP、真实浏览器管理、Metrics/OTel 和故障恢复。附录 A1 补充自定义组件与真实存储。
144	
145	本课的 Agent 是独立 Python 进程，Receiver 在同机通过真实 HTTP 运行；不包含 Codex / Claude Code
146	原生插件交互、跨机器网络部署、集群高可用或性能基准。OpenDAL 展示当前 Connector 的实际接入能力，
147	没有把材料扫描等同于自动生成 Memory。各篇会分别检查来源、状态、版本与实际执行结果。
148	
149	## 独立 HTTP 专题
150	
151	第 11 篇只用 `httpx` 和普通 JSON，不需要 PowerContext Python Client，也不需要模型。
152	它从一个真实金额精度问题的 Source 开始，直接创建四类制品，再读取、分页、更新和检查历史。
153	每步展示可展开的请求与响应 JSON，以及有用的响应头；不显示 Authorization。
154	
155	在**仓库根目录的终端 A** 启动教学 Server，并保持运行：
156	
157	```bash
158	POWERCONTEXT_HOME="$PWD/examples/jupyter/.powercontext/http-server" \
159	  uv run --locked --group notebooks powercontext server run \
160	  --env-file examples/jupyter/server-http.env.example --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000
161	```
162	
163	该配置使用独立 SQLite，不启用模型或后台提取。`--env-file` 会替换继承的 `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_*` 设置。
164	在**终端 B** 运行 `make notebooks`，打开第 11 篇。换用其他端口时，同时设置
165	`POWERCONTEXT_CLIENT_SERVER_URL`。连接已有远程服务时使用 HTTPS，将凭据放在
166	`POWERCONTEXT_CLIENT_API_TOKEN` 中。调用者需要创建新 Scope 及读写权限。
167	
168	也可以保持 Server 运行，在终端 B 完整执行并保留输出：
169	
170	```bash
171	make notebooks-test ARGS="--with-http --only 11"
172	```
173	
174	成功时会观察到四类制品的创建与读取，真实的 `304` 条件读取、`412` 过期版本条件、
175	`428` 缺少更新条件，以及制品搜索结果。
176	最后一个单元格关闭应用连接；在终端 A 按 **Ctrl+C** 停止 Server。
177	
178	## 配置模型篇
179	
180	在仓库根目录复制配置模板，再用编辑器填写。不要把 Key 写进 Notebook：
181	
182	```bash
183	cp examples/jupyter/.env.example examples/jupyter/.env
184	```
185	
186	模板使用 OpenAI-compatible 协议，模型名和 Embedding 维度应填写自己服务的实际值。
187	`OPENAI_API_KEY` / `OPENAI_BASE_URL` 表示兼容连接配置，不限定供应商。
188	
189	| 配置 | 用途 |
190	| --- | --- |
191	| `OPENAI_API_KEY`、`OPENAI_BASE_URL` | 服务凭据与 API base URL；本地服务按其要求填写 |
192	| `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_INFERENCE_GENERATION_MODEL` | 08、09、15–17、21–22 的生成模型，例如 `openai-chat:<实际模型名>` |
193	| `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_INFERENCE_EMBEDDING_MODEL` | 09 的向量模型，例如 `openai:<实际模型名>` |
194	| `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_INFERENCE_EMBEDDING_PROFILE_ID` | 稳定的向量配置标识，更换模型或维度时更换相应标识 |
195	| `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_INFERENCE_EMBEDDING_DIMENSION` | 模型实际输出维度 |
196	| `POWERCONTEXT_NOTEBOOK_CHAT_MODEL` | 10、16、19、21–22 的对话模型名，不带 provider 前缀，需支持工具调用 |
197	
198	已有可用配置时，可在启动 Jupyter 前指定它的**绝对路径**：
199	
200	```bash
201	export POWERCONTEXT_NOTEBOOK_ENV_FILE=/absolute/path/to/your/.env
202	make notebooks
203	```
204	
205	辅助代码只把需要的 inference 设置用于本篇独立 Server，不继承文件里的业务数据库和定时任务；
206	OceanBase 仅由上面的专用 Notebook 配置项显式启用。
207	第 10 篇由 Agent 直接调用模型，PowerContext Server 仍不需要模型；未单独配置 chat 模型时，
208	可从 `openai-chat:` 或 `openai:` generation 配置中读取模型名。
209	
210	已设置的环境变量优先于 `.env`。修改配置后，重启 Kernel 并从头运行。
211	配置不足会明确停止，不会用测试模型代替。真实服务调用会产生相应用量：
212	生成篇实际提取、生成或改进候选；09 计算向量并调用 rerank；Agent 篇会有多轮真实对话和工具调用。
213	复杂生成可配置 `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_INFERENCE_GENERATION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=120`；执行器的 `--timeout` 应大于单次服务超时。
214	
215	## 读懂结果
216	
217	Source 是原始材料，保存成功不代表已成为 Memory。Atomic Memory 的 `forgotten` 状态使该记忆退出 active recall，
218	正文与历史版本仍可读取，状态版本的变化不会增加正文 revision。准备上下文、加入模型请求、模型实际采用它，是需要分别观察的三个结果。
219	
220	Handoff 的准备预览、提交、接收确认和任务完成也是不同状态。05 先预览再 commit，两个 Client 模拟交接角色；
221	10、16、19、21、22 调用真实 Agent。Skill 的创建或批准不会自动在 Host 中安装、发现或执行它；07 实际导出文件，17 验证完整包，18 与 22 用 Receiver 实际安装和撤回。
222

245	`passed` 表示整篇运行通过；`failed` 会保留失败处输出；`not_run` 表示未启用模型、浏览器或独立 HTTP 服务，不能当作通过。

253	分别验证无鉴权与 Bearer；每次执行无论成功或失败，都停止 Server、删除临时凭证文件，并仅删除本次成功创建的数据库。

255	需要具有创建和删除测试库权限的配置。显式执行开关代表允许该脚本创建与清理自己的临时库；它不删除既有数据库。
256	管理员连接来自所选配置文件中的 `POWERCONTEXT_NOTEBOOK_OCEANBASE_ADMIN_URL`，未设置时使用 `POWERCONTEXT_SERVER_DATABASE_URL`。
257	模型配置也从该文件读取，具体值不会写入验证清单。

261	  --env-file /absolute/path/to/test.env --create-temporary-databases
262

269	`deleted: true` 表示已查询确认数据库不存在。失败不是通过；检查 `returncode`、`execution` 和保留的实际异常。

271	## 常见问题
272	
273	| 现象 | 检查方式 |
274	| --- | --- |
275	| 找不到包、Jupyter 或适配器 | 重新执行 `uv sync --locked --group notebooks`，确认 Kernel 来自同一环境 |
276	| 提示事件循环已运行 | 单元格使用顶层 `await`，无需 `asyncio.run()` |
277	| 首格不能监听端口 | Kernel 和 Server 需要回环 socket；检查运行环境是否允许本地端口 |
278	| 单独重跑写入或审核格出现冲突 | 使用 Restart Kernel & Run All；精确引用与已经终结的候选不能任意重放 |
279	| 模型配置缺失或调用失败 | 检查配置路径、模型、兼容地址、维度和服务可达性，然后重启 Kernel |
280	| 模型没有得到预期结果 | 阅读实际 Source、候选或模型输入；不要手工补写结果掩盖模型行为验收失败 |
281	| 08 生成 Skill 返回 500，日志提示名称非法 | 生成接口不接收调用方指定名称；检查模型输出的 name 是否为小写英文、数字与单连字符，保留实际失败结果 |
282	| Skill 导出提示目录已存在 | CLI 不覆盖已有目录；检查导出，或完整重跑建立新实验 |
283	| Agent 回答没用上知识 | 先看回调捕获的实际模型输入，再区分召回、Scope 选择与模型采用问题 |
284	| HTTP 连接失败或返回 401 / 403 | 检查独立 Server、地址、token 与权限；Notebook 末尾附状态码说明 |

1	---
2	title: HTTP API
3	description: Call the PowerContext Server over HTTP and find the complete OpenAPI contract.

5	
6	# HTTP API
7	
8	The HTTP API is the language-neutral interface to a running PowerContext Server. The default base URL is
9	`http://127.0.0.1:8000`.
10	
11	If you are integrating PowerContext into your own AI application rather than looking up one field, start with the
12	[HTTP API lifecycle tutorial](api-quickstart.md). This page remains the path, contract, and
13	error-semantics reference.
14	
15	## Discover the contract
16	
17	With a local unauthenticated Server running, open `/docs` for the interactive Scalar API reference or
18	`/openapi.json` for the contract served by that process.
19	
20	The checked-in source of truth is
21	[`openapi/powercontext.yaml`](https://github.com/oceanbase/powercontext/blob/master/openapi/powercontext.yaml).
22	Use it when generating a client or reviewing every request and response field. `/docs` remains public when Server
23	authentication is enabled so it can render the reference; requests made from it still require authentication.
24	`/openapi.json` requires the bearer token. A browser address bar cannot add that header, so use a trusted proxy or
25	browser setup that injects it, or download `/openapi.json` with an authenticated command. Never put the token in the
26	URL.
27	
28	## Authenticate requests
29	
30	Authentication is disabled for the default loopback-only installation. When the operator enables it, include this
31	header on API and MCP requests:
32	
33	```http
34	Authorization: Bearer <token>
35	```
36	
37	The examples below use an optional shell variable:
38	
39	```bash
40	POWERCONTEXT_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000
41	POWERCONTEXT_AUTH_HEADER="Authorization: Bearer ${POWERCONTEXT_CLIENT_API_TOKEN}"
42	```
43	
44	Omit `--header "$POWERCONTEXT_AUTH_HEADER"` when authentication is disabled. The `/health/live` and
45	`/health/ready` endpoints are always public. See [Deploy the Server](../operate/deploy-server.md) before allowing remote
46	access.
47	
48	For an authenticated Server, download the exact contract served by that process with:
49	
50	```bash
51	curl --fail \
52	  --header "$POWERCONTEXT_AUTH_HEADER" \
53	  --output powercontext-openapi.json \
54	  "$POWERCONTEXT_URL/openapi.json"
55	```
56	
57	## Store and search one Memory
58	
59	Set `POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID` to an existing ID returned by `create_scope`. Reuse that Scope across sessions; a Session
60	ID is not a durable project identity.
61	
62	Store one already-curated Memory entry:
63	
64	```bash
65	curl --fail \
66	  --request POST \
67	  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
68	  --header "$POWERCONTEXT_AUTH_HEADER" \
69	  --data "{
70	    \"scope_id\": \"${POWERCONTEXT_SCOPE_ID}\",
71	    \"kind\": \"decision\",
72	    \"text\": \"Keep the public API asynchronous.\"
73	  }" \
74	  "$POWERCONTEXT_URL/v1/memory/remember"
75	```
76	
77	The response's `records` contain independent memories with an `artifact` reference, content, state, and `state_version`.
78	Read history by exact ArtifactRef, edit through the generic Artifact API with its content ETag, and use Atomic Memory
79	operations to forget or restore. See [Atomic Memory](../workflows/atomic-memory.md) for complete examples.
