**Response:**

1	# Qoder environment
2	
3	## Project and source
4	
5	Use the current task's original local project. Preserve its instructions, package manager, lockfile, source layout and installed components. For a new simple static site, create a dedicated public directory such as `web/` or `dist/`; a framework is optional. Existing applications keep their own setup and build scripts.
6	
7	For a new React application in an empty project, use the bundled [React starter](../templates/react-starter/README.md). Run `node "<sites-building-skill-root>/scripts/create-site.mjs" "<project-root>"` with the real installed skill and current project paths. The initializer checks the starter's supported Node versions before copying and refuses to overwrite existing project files. The starter includes a typed UI catalog, static build and optional local Function proxy, but does not allocate cloud resources.
8	
9	Run `npm ci` from the new project root, then `npm run dev` after installation succeeds. While installation runs, author independent page and style changes without modifying dependency inputs. Use one installer at a time and reuse the installed dependencies while the manifest and lockfile remain unchanged. Existing applications use their own package manager and scripts; plain static sites need no installation.
10	
11	Before cloud preparation, use `get_context.local` to read the current conversation directory. Both registered workspaces and standalone local conversations are supported. Build and prepare inside that directory; the host verifies it. Remote sessions are unsupported. To work elsewhere, open a conversation there and reuse the existing site IDs.
12	
13	The generated `.站点名称.qoder.site` is a publication-preview descriptor, not editable hosting configuration or a source repository. It points to a prepared action and cannot recover missing source on another machine. Use the tools to write or refresh it. Reuse Project/Site/action IDs from the current task and tool results; if the intended site is ambiguous, resolve it with `list_sites` and `get_site` before writing.
14	
15	## Local tools
16	
17	Check only tools needed for the selected workflow, using their version command before first use. Resolve assets from the installed skill directory and browser/image tools from the current tool catalog. A development checkout, personal utility, or a plugin's private runtime is not assumed to be available on the task's PATH.
18	
19	- **Node/npm:** Existing projects keep their own engine requirement. For a new React project, use the starter's [package.json](../templates/react-starter/package.json) and bundled version check. Select an already installed compatible runtime through the environment's available version manager or its verified executable path. Keep that runtime selected for install, dev and build in subsequent commands; do not change global shell profiles. Plain static files need no Node installation.
20	- **Static preview:** Prefer the project's existing preview command or an available host preview. The [Python server example](static-site.md#local-preview-and-acceptance) is an optional fallback. If no server is available, finish file/build checks and independent work; report local preview as unavailable. A local-only delivery still needs a usable preview before claiming it complete.
21	- **Function checks:** Use the project's existing check when it validates the same Edge Function entry and imports. Otherwise check `deno --version` before `deno check` or local Deno execution. If Deno is unavailable, continue frontend work and credential-free handler checks on an available runtime; keep Deno-specific validation pending until a compatible local runtime or an existing CI check can run it. Frontend TypeScript checks and Node fixtures do not prove Deno compatibility.
22	- **HTTPS examples:** Reuse a suitable local certificate and key. Certificate generation requires an available tool; follow the [HTTPS example](../assets/application-auth/README.md#local-https) for alternatives. Keep certificate files outside upload roots.
23	
24	Use the environment's normal dependency setup when a required tool is missing. Record the exact missing tool and affected check if setup is unavailable; tool absence does not justify changing the requested feature, weakening TLS/authentication, or reporting a check as passed. HTTP probes can use the available request tool, Node `fetch`, or `curl`; no particular HTTP CLI is required.
25	
26	## Runtime and output
27	
28	| Concern | Qoder Sites implementation |
29	| --- | --- |
30	| Website | Static files with `index.html` in the selected `webDirectory` |
31	| SPA | `prepare_site.spa`; use the current routing contract in [Static output](static-site.md) |
32	| Server code | One `app` Edge Function from `functionDirectory/index.ts`; HTTP handler with self-contained imports |
33	| Browser-to-server calls | Same-origin `/functions/v1/app`, including supported subpaths/query actions |
34	| Database | Function-side Supabase adapter, reviewed schema/access policies and explicit runtime dependency |
35	| Files | Platform Storage through the injected Function SDK; owner file tools are separate administration |
36	| AI | [sites-build-agent-app](../../sites-build-agent-app/SKILL.md), using the `qoder` backend and server-only `QODER_PAT` |
37	| Draft and release | `prepare_site`, `get_publish_status`, `publish_site`; Desktop preview uses its MCP only |
38	
39	Use [Functions and Secrets](functions.md) before server implementation. Do not assume a Node server, framework SSR process, Cloudflare Worker binding or deployment-time package installation is available. The packager does not compile application source or resolve missing dependencies. Framework applications must produce compatible static output and put required server behavior behind the Function.
40	
41	Qoder supplies the current account and management authentication. Use the available `sites` tools, not a new management HTTP client or user-pasted PAT. Ordinary application Secret values are entered through Sites Settings; only names enter preparation inputs. Reserved platform Secrets remain inside their supported runtime boundaries.
42	
43	## Local preview and testing
44	
45	When needed for a supported preview or local validation, start the project's existing development or preview command in a retained process, bound to loopback. For plain static files, use the local server example in [Static output](static-site.md). For Functions, use the same-origin development proxy and local handler setup in [Functions and Secrets](functions.md). Keep local fixture servers outside both output directories.
46	
47	Read the server's actual URL and make a lightweight request to confirm the route serves successfully. A response check proves availability, not visual or functional correctness. Fix compilation or blocking runtime errors before presenting the first meaningful preview.
48	
49	For a foreground task, inspect the available user-facing preview tool's current schema. If it supports this local URL, show the [first meaningful preview](../SKILL.md#first-meaningful-preview) and retain the returned view identity for later updates. Reuse the same view and server rather than opening another development window. No particular browser or preview API is assumed. Without a supported tool, continue implementation; provide the verified URL for local-only work or an explicit request to see development progress. Hosted work finishes with the publication preview described below. Browser QA follows the [validation scope](../SKILL.md#validation-scope), independently of opening a preview.
50	
51	Keep the server available while the user is reviewing or continuing local work. Record the PID and working directory of each server you start. Stop only that verified task-owned process when no longer needed; choose another free port when occupied. Do not kill processes by port, broad name patterns or unverified PID lists, even to recover a failed preview. Background work can skip a user-facing preview and still validate the output.
52	
53	## Hosted delivery
54	
55	Read [sites-hosting](../../sites-hosting/SKILL.md) after the requested website is complete and locally validated. That skill owns publication, draft recovery and preview handoff, including explicit requests to defer publication. `prepare_site` can create a project, allocate requested backend capabilities and upload artifacts; it is not a local preview command. Source commits, pushes and manually assembled outer manifests/archives are not publication prerequisites.
56	
57	On Desktop, the Canvas preview renders the exact frozen static package. CLI returns the published site link or a deferred draft summary and does not open a Canvas. The preview does not execute Functions, supply website credentials or prove backend behavior. The final release and its access policy remain server-owned. Follow `sites-hosting` to publish by default and then show the preview; audience changes retain their separate authorization requirements.
58	
59	
60	Desktop `get_context.local` discovers `.qoder.site` files under the current workspace. Choose the intended site directory before preparation. Keep one site file per directory; multiple sites in one workspace use separate subdirectories. Preparation automatically reuses the current account’s verified directory binding. For an explicit request to create a new site for another account in the same source directory, follow [account switching](../../sites-hosting/references/deployment.md#switching-accounts-in-the-same-source-directory). Do not copy or edit a site file to create another site or change its publication identity. The file embeds the frozen homepage; original source and build output remain in the project.

## seekdb Vector Search API -- Complete Reference

**Project location:** `C:\Users\ouyan\Desktop\oceanbase\seekdb-update` (note: the exact path `seekdb` does not exist; the project is at `seekdb-update`)

**Online docs:** https://docs.seekdb.ai/

---

### 1. Creating Tables with Vector Columns

**Dense vector column type:** `VECTOR(dimensions)`

```sql
-- Basic: vector column with inline HNSW index
CREATE TABLE articles (
  id        INT PRIMARY KEY,
  title     TEXT,
  content   TEXT,
  embedding VECTOR(384),
  FULLTEXT INDEX idx_fts (content) WITH PARSER ik,
  VECTOR   INDEX idx_vec (embedding) WITH (DISTANCE=l2, TYPE=hnsw, LIB=vsag)
) ORGANIZATION = HEAP;
```

**Key constraints from test files:**
- Vector dimensions: 1 to 4096 (4097 and 0 are rejected)
- Only ONE column per vector index (multi-column vector indexes are rejected)
- Cannot have two vector indexes on the same column
- `ORGANIZATION = HEAP` is supported (and used in examples)

**Vector index types supported:**
| Type | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `hnsw` | HNSW graph index (vsag library) |
| `hnsw_sq` | HNSW with scalar quantization |
| `hnsw_bq` | HNSW with binary quantization |
| `ivf_flat` | IVF with flat quantizer (ob library) |
| `ivf_sq8` | IVF with 8-bit scalar quantization |
| `ivf_pq` | IVF with product quantization |
| `sindi` | Sparse vector index (vsag library) |

**Distance metrics:**
| Distance | Similarity Function | Order-by Function |
|----------|-------------------|-------------------|
| `l2` | `l2_similarity(col, vec)` | `l2_distance(col, vec)` |
| `cosine` | `cosine_similarity(col, vec)` | `cosine_distance(col, vec)` |
| `inner_product` | `inner_product_similarity(col, vec)` | `negative_inner_product(col, vec)` |

**HNSW index parameters:**
```sql
VECTOR INDEX idx1(col) WITH (
  distance=l2,          -- l2 | cosine | inner_product
  type=hnsw,            -- hnsw | hnsw_sq | hnsw_bq
  lib=vsag,             -- vsag (default for hnsw)
  m=20,                 -- [1,128], connections per node
  ef_construction=200,  -- [m, 1000], build quality
  ef_search=200         -- [1, 160000], search quality
)
```

**IVF index parameters:**
```sql
VECTOR INDEX idx1(col) WITH (
  distance=l2,
  type=ivf_flat,        -- ivf_flat | ivf_sq8 | ivf_pq
  lib=ob,
  nlist=2,              -- number of clusters
  sample_per_nlist=3    -- samples per cluster for training
)
-- For ivf_pq, add: m=1  (number of sub-quantizers)
```

**Sparse vector column type:** `SPARSEVECTOR` (no dimension needed)

```sql
CREATE TABLE t1 (
  c1 INT PRIMARY KEY,
  c2 SPARSEVECTOR,
  VECTOR INDEX idx1(c2) WITH (
    lib=vsag,
    type=sindi,
    distance=inner_product,
    prune=true,            -- enable pruning
    refine=true,           -- enable refinement
    drop_ratio_build=0.1,  -- [0.0, 0.9]
    drop_ratio_search=0.5, -- [0.0, 0.9]
    refine_k=2.0           -- [1.0, 1000.0]
  )
);
```

**Hybrid vector index (auto-embedding from text):**
```sql
-- First, create AI model and endpoint
CALL DBMS_AI_SERVICE.CREATE_AI_MODEL('ob_embed', '{
    "type": "dense_embedding",
    "model_name": "bge-M3"
}');
CALL DBMS_AI_SERVICE.CREATE_AI_MODEL_ENDPOINT('ob_embed_endpoint', '{
    "ai_model_name": "ob_embed",
    "scope": "all",
    "url": "https://your-endpoint/",
    "access_key": "sk-xxx",
    "request_model_name": "bge-M3",
    "provider": "openai"
}');

-- Then create index on VARCHAR column (not TEXT, not STRING)
CREATE TABLE t_vec (
  c1 INT PRIMARY KEY,
  c2 VARCHAR(100),
  VECTOR INDEX vec_idx1(c2) WITH (
    distance=l2,
    type=hnsw,
    model=ob_embed,       -- AI model name
    dim=1024,             -- embedding dimension
    sync_mode=immediate   -- immediate | async | manual
    -- For async: sync_interval=20s
  )
);
```

**Partitioned tables with vector indexes are supported:**
```sql
CREATE TABLE t1 (
  c1 INT, c2 VECTOR(3), PRIMARY KEY(c1),
  VECTOR INDEX idx1(c2) WITH (distance=L2, type=hnsw, lib=vsag, m=20, ef_construction=200, ef_search=200)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (c1) (
  PARTITION p0 VALUES LESS THAN (20),
  PARTITION p1 VALUES LESS THAN (40)
);

-- Also: PARTITION BY HASH(c1) PARTITIONS 4
```

---

### 2. Inserting Vectors

**Dense vectors** -- as JSON array strings or bare arrays:

```sql
-- String format (quoted)
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(1, '[0.203846,0.205289,0.880265]');

-- Array format (unquoted, also works)
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(1, [1,1,1]);

-- Multiple rows
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1, '[1,2,3]'), (2, '[3,4,5]');

-- Upsert
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1, '[1,2,3]') ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE c2 = '[1,2,3]';

-- Update vector
UPDATE t1 SET c2 = [0.615,0.613,0.031] WHERE c1 = 3;

-- NULL vectors are allowed (unless column is NOT NULL)
INSERT INTO t1(c1, c2) VALUES (2, '[0.99,0.86,0.11]');  -- c3 is NULL
```

**Sparse vectors** -- as JSON-like key-value strings:

```sql
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(1, '{1:0.1, 2:0.2, 3:0.3}');
INSERT INTO t1 VALUES(2, '{3:0.3, 2:0.2, 4:0.4}');
```

---

### 3. Vector Similarity Search (ANN Search)

**Core pattern:** `ORDER BY <distance_function>(col, target_vec) APPROXIMATE LIMIT n`

The `APPROXIMATE` keyword triggers ANN (approximate nearest neighbor) search using the vector index.

**L2 distance search:**
```sql
SELECT * FROM t1
ORDER BY l2_distance(c2, [0.712,0.603,0.133]) APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 5;
```

**Cosine distance search:**
```sql
SELECT c1, cosine_similarity(c3, [1,1,2])
FROM t1
ORDER BY cosine_distance(c3, [1,1,2]) APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 5;
```

**Inner product search (for sparse vectors):**
```sql
SELECT * FROM t1
ORDER BY negative_inner_product(c2, '{3:1, 4:1}') APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 2;
```

**With scalar filter (pre-filter):**
```sql
SELECT * FROM t1
WHERE c0 = 2
ORDER BY l2_distance(c2, [0.712,0.603,0.133]) APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 10;
```

**With similarity threshold parameter:**
```sql
-- For L2: similarity must be > threshold
SELECT c1, l2_similarity(c3, [1,1,2])
FROM t1
ORDER BY l2_distance(c3, [1,1,2]) APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 5 PARAMETERS (similarity=0.0001);

-- For cosine: similarity must be > threshold
SELECT c1, cosine_similarity(c3, [1,1,2])
FROM t1
ORDER BY cosine_distance(c3, [1,1,2]) APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 5 PARAMETERS (similarity=0.7);

-- For sparse vectors: refine_k and drop_ratio_search
SELECT * FROM t1
WHERE c1 <= 5
ORDER BY negative_inner_product(c2, '{3:1, 4:1}') APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 2 PARAMETERS (refine_k=1.0, drop_ratio_search=0.2);
```

**Hybrid search: vector + full-text in one SQL:**
```sql
SELECT id, title,
       l2_distance(embedding, '[0.12, 0.34, ...]') AS dist
FROM articles
WHERE MATCH(content) AGAINST('quarterly report')
  AND author_id = 42
  AND created_at > '2026-01-01'
ORDER BY dist APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 10;
```

**Exact (brute-force) search** -- omit `APPROXIMATE`:
```sql
SELECT c1, l2_distance(c2, [0.765,0.560,0.900]) AS l2
FROM t1
ORDER BY l2
LIMIT 5;
```

**Force index hint:**
```sql
SELECT /*+index(t1 idx1)*/ * FROM t1
WHERE c2 > 3
ORDER BY l2_distance(c3, [1,1,2]) APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 5 PARAMETERS (similarity=0.0001);
```

**Offset support:**
```sql
SELECT * FROM t1
ORDER BY l2_distance(c4, [1,2,3]) APPROXIMATE
LIMIT 1, 1;   -- offset 1, count 1
```

---

### 4. Index Maintenance (dbms_vector Package)

**Incremental refresh (delta index update):**
```sql
CALL dbms_vector.refresh_index('idx1', 'table_name', 'vector_col', 1, 'FAST');
-- Parameters: index_name, table_name, column_name, refresh_threshold, refresh_type
```

**Full rebuild:**
```sql
CALL dbms_vector.rebuild_index('idx1', 'table_name', 'vector_col');
-- Parameters: index_name, table_name, column_name, delta_rate_threshold, ...
```

**Memory estimation:**
```sql
SELECT dbms_vector.index_vector_memory_advisor('hnsw', 1000000, 384, 'FLOAT32');
SELECT dbms_vector.index_vector_memory_estimate('table_name', 'col_name', 'hnsw');
```

---

### 5. Python SDK: pyseekdb

**Installation:**
```bash
pip install -U pyseekdb
```

**PyPI package name:** `pyseekdb`

**Usage (embedded mode):**
```python
import pyseekdb

client = pyseekdb.Client(path="./agent_state.db")
memory = client.get_or_create_collection(name="episodic")

# Insert/upsert
memory.upsert(
    ids=["1", "2", "3"],
    documents=[
        "user prefers dark mode",
        "user speaks English and Chinese",
        "user timezone is UTC+8",
    ],
)
memory.refresh_index()

# Query
results = memory.query(query_texts="ui preferences?", n_results=2)
# Returns: {"documents": [[...]], "distances": [[...]]}
```

The Python SDK auto-handles embedding, index management, and provides a Chroma-like API. Full docs at https://github.com/oceanbase/pyseekdb.

---

### 6. FORK/MERGE Sandbox (COW)

```sql
-- Snapshot database (no data copy)
FORK DATABASE agent_state TO agent_sandbox_42;

-- Work in sandbox
USE agent_sandbox_42;
INSERT INTO memory (session_id, embedding, content) VALUES (...);

-- Merge back (strategies: FAIL / THEIRS / OURS)
MERGE TABLE agent_sandbox_42.memory INTO agent_state.memory STRATEGY THEIRS;

-- Or discard
DROP DATABASE agent_sandbox_42;
```

94	
95	<a id="why-seekdb-for-agents"></a>
96	
97	## ✨ Why seekdb for Agents?
98	
99	### 🔥 Streaming Write + Concurrent Search, Without the P99 Spike
100	
101	Agent workloads are continuous write + millisecond-later read. seekdb's
102	**async index pipeline (Change Stream)** decouples DML from index build,
103	and its **two-level HNSW** (incremental + snapshot) makes newly-written
104	vectors immediately searchable.
105	
106	<div align="center">
107	  <img src="images/architecture.svg" alt="seekdb async index pipeline architecture" width="720" />
108	</div>
109	
110	The write path commits and returns *without waiting* on index construction.
111	The Change Stream pipeline consumes the redo log asynchronously and updates
112	the delta HNSW. Queries hit both delta and snapshot indexes with fine-grained
113	read locks — **this is why P99 stays flat under concurrency.**
114	
115	> **The result: 1,523 QPS with 21.7 ms concurrent P99 — 10.7× the QPS of
116	> Milvus, and P99 jitter of just 1.1× when concurrency rises (vs ~10×
117	> for ES / Milvus on the same workload).**
118	
119	<sub>Source: [`src/observer/change_stream/`](src/observer/change_stream/) · [`src/observer/vector_index/`](src/observer/vector_index/)</sub>
120	
121	### 🌿 Copy-on-Write Sandboxes for Agent Exploration
122	
123	`FORK DATABASE` snapshots an entire database in seconds — no data copy.
124	Agents experiment freely (write, query, even break tables); then `MERGE TABLE`
125	commits the work back, or `DROP DATABASE` discards it. Kernel-level COW,
126	not application-layer save/restore.
127	
128	```sql
129	-- Snapshot in seconds, no data copy
130	FORK DATABASE agent_state TO agent_sandbox_42;
131	
132	-- Agent reads/writes freely on the sandbox...
133	USE agent_sandbox_42;
134	INSERT INTO memory (session_id, embedding, content) VALUES (...);
135	
136	-- Accept the work back to mainline (strategies: FAIL / THEIRS / OURS)
137	MERGE TABLE agent_sandbox_42.memory INTO agent_state.memory STRATEGY THEIRS;
138	-- ...or throw it away:
139	DROP DATABASE agent_sandbox_42;
140	```
141	
142	<sub>Source: [`tools/deploy/mysql_test/test_suite/fork_table/`](tools/deploy/mysql_test/test_suite/fork_table/)</sub>
143	
144	### 🔍 Hybrid Search in a Single SQL
145	
146	Vector + full-text + scalar filter pushed into one execution plan.
147	No N+1 client-side merging, no glue code to combine results.
148	
149	```sql
150	SELECT id, title, l2_distance(emb, '[0.12,0.34,...]') AS dist
151	FROM docs
152	WHERE MATCH(content) AGAINST('quarterly report')
153	  AND author_id = 42
154	  AND created_at > '2026-01-01'
155	ORDER BY dist APPROXIMATE LIMIT 10;
156	```
157	
158	### 🐬 MySQL-Compatible, ACID, Embeddable
159	
160	Built on the proven OceanBase SQL engine. Works as an embedded library,
161	a single-node server, or in the OceanBase distributed cluster. Full ACID,
162	real-time writes, and the entire MySQL ecosystem out of the box.
163

227	<a id="more-examples"></a>
228	
229	### 📝 More Examples
230	
231	For the full Python SDK walkthrough — connection modes, embedding functions, metadata filters — see the [pyseekdb User Guide](https://github.com/oceanbase/pyseekdb).
232	
233	<details open>
234	<summary><b>🤖 Agent Memory Pattern (continuous write + immediate retrieval)</b></summary>
235	
236	The canonical agent loop: write an observation, retrieve relevant context
237	milliseconds later, repeat. seekdb's async index pipeline keeps both
238	sides fast under sustained concurrency.
239	
240	```python
241	import pyseekdb
242	
243	client = pyseekdb.Client(path="./agent_state.db")
244	memory = client.get_or_create_collection(name="episodic")
245	
246	for step in agent.run():
247	    # Persist the observation
248	    memory.upsert(ids=[step.id], documents=[step.observation])
249	
250	    # Retrieve relevant context — milliseconds after the write,
251	    # served by the incremental HNSW (no waiting on a background rebuild)
252	    relevant = memory.query(query_texts=step.next_query, n_results=5)
253	
254	    agent.act(relevant)
255	```
256	
257	</details>
258	
259	<details>
260	<summary><b>🗄️ SQL — Schema + Hybrid Search</b></summary>
261	
262	```sql
263	-- Table with vector column, full-text index, and HNSW vector index
264	CREATE TABLE articles (
265	  id        INT PRIMARY KEY,
266	  title     TEXT,
267	  content   TEXT,
268	  embedding VECTOR(384),
269	  FULLTEXT INDEX idx_fts (content) WITH PARSER ik,
270	  VECTOR   INDEX idx_vec (embedding) WITH (DISTANCE=l2, TYPE=hnsw, LIB=vsag)
271	) ORGANIZATION = HEAP;
272	
273	-- Hybrid search: vector similarity + full-text match in one query
274	SELECT id, title,
275	       l2_distance(embedding, '[0.12, 0.34, ...]') AS dist
276	FROM articles
277	WHERE MATCH(content) AGAINST('quarterly report')
278	ORDER BY dist APPROXIMATE
279	LIMIT 10;
280	```
281	
282	Python developers can access this via SQLAlchemy or any MySQL driver.
283	
284	</details>
285	
286	
287	## 📚 Use Cases
288	
289	<details open>
290	<summary><b>🎯 Agentic AI — Memory, Sandbox & State</b></summary>
291	
292	Agents need a state store that handles continuous memory writes,
293	millisecond-later retrieval, branching for exploration, and rollback when
294	things go wrong. seekdb is built for exactly this:
295	
296	- **Streaming-friendly storage** — write a memory, query it in the next ms
297	- **COW sandboxes** — `FORK DATABASE` for safe experimentation, `MERGE` to accept, `DROP` to roll back
298	- **Hybrid retrieval** — vector + full-text + relational in one SQL
299	- **MySQL protocol** — works with LangChain, LlamaIndex, Dify out of the box
300	
301	Personal assistants · Enterprise automation · Vertical agents · Agent platforms
302	
303	</details>
304	
305	<details>
306	<summary><b>🧩 Other Use Cases</b></summary>
307	
308	seekdb's hybrid retrieval + multi-model engine also fits classic AI workloads:
309	
310	- **📖 RAG & Knowledge Retrieval** — vector + full-text + scalar filters with multi-level access control. *Enterprise QA, customer support, industry insights, personal knowledge bases.*
311	- **🔍 Semantic Search** — embedding-based search across text, images, and other modalities. *Product search, text-to-image, image-to-product.*
312	- **💻 AI-Assisted Coding** — semantic code search, multi-project isolation, time-travel queries for IDE plugins and code agents. *Local IDEs, web IDEs, design-to-web.*
313	- **⬆️ Enterprise Application Intelligence** — MySQL-compatible AI layer for legacy systems, with row/column hybrid storage. *Document intelligence, business insights, finance systems.*
314	- **📱 On-Device & Edge AI** — embedded / micro-server modes for resource-constrained devices. *In-vehicle systems, AI education, companion robots, healthcare devices.*
315	
316	</details>

380	## 🛠️ Development
381	
382	### Build from Source
383	
384	Before building, please install the required toolchain and dependencies for your operating system. See [Install Toolchain](docs/developer-guide/en/toolchain.md) for detailed instructions.
385	
386	```bash
387	# Clone the repository
388	git clone https://github.com/oceanbase/seekdb.git
389	cd seekdb
390	./build.sh release --init --make
391	mkdir -p ~/seekdb/bin
392	cp build_release/src/observer/seekdb ~/seekdb/bin
393	cd ~/seekdb
394	./bin/seekdb
395	```
396	
397	In this example, the working directory is $HOME/seekdb, please use a fresh directory for testing. Please see the [Developer Guide](docs/developer-guide/en/README.md) for detailed instructions.
398

**Response:**

1	import pyseekdb
2	
3	client = pyseekdb.Client(path="./agent_state.db")
4	memory = client.get_or_create_collection(name="episodic")
5	
6	# Round 1: write Agent observations
7	print("--- Round 1: write 3 observations ---")
8	memory.upsert(
9	    ids=["1", "2", "3"],
10	    documents=[
11	        "user prefers dark mode",
12	        "user speaks English and Chinese",
13	        "user timezone is UTC+8",
14	    ],
15	)
16	memory.refresh_index()
17	
18	print('Query: "ui preferences?"')
19	results = memory.query(query_texts="ui preferences?", n_results=2)
20	for doc, dist in zip(results["documents"][0], results["distances"][0]):
21	    print(f"  {doc}  (distance: {dist:.4f})")
22	
23	# Round 2: write new observation, queryable immediately
24	print("\n--- Round 2: write 1 new observation ---")
25	memory.upsert(ids=["4"], documents=["user saw pricing page 3 times today"])
26	memory.refresh_index()
27	
28	print('Query: "purchase intent signals"')
29	results = memory.query(query_texts="purchase intent signals", n_results=1)
30	print("Result:", results["documents"])
31

342	create table normal(c1 int, c2 vector(3), primary key(c1));
343	CREATE VIEW vec_view as select * from vec;

507	drop procedure if exists insert_data;
508	delimiter |;
509	create PROCEDURE insert_data(cnt int)
510	BEGIN
511	  DECLARE i INT DEFAULT 0;
512		WHILE i < cnt do
513	    insert into ai_knowledge_new(record_id, knowledge_base_id, content_vector) values (cast(i as char), 176, @test_vec);
514			set i = i + 1;
515		end while;
516	END |
517	delimiter ;|
518	
519	call insert_data(50);
520	
521	SELECT  /*+PARALLEL(8) */ cosine_similarity(`content_vector`, @test_vec )=0 FROM ai_knowledge_new  ak where knowledge_base_id in ('176') order BY cosine_distance(`content_vector`, @test_vec ) APPROXIMATE LIMIT 100;
522	
523	--disable_warnings
524	drop table if exists ai_knowledge_new;
525	drop procedure insert_data;
526	drop table if exists t1;
527	--enable_warnings

50	MySQL 兼容 · 嵌入式 / 服务器双模式 · 向量 + 全文混合搜索 · COW 沙箱
51	
52	⚡ 流式写入 + 搜索达 1,523 QPS（10× Milvus，3× Elasticsearch）<br>
53	🌿 FORK/MERGE 沙箱，Agent 安全探索、自由回滚<br>
54	🔍 向量 + 全文 + 标量，一条 SQL 搞定<br>
55	🐬 完整 ACID，MySQL 协议，原生对接 LangChain / LlamaIndex / Dify

pyseekdb.Client(path=settings.seekdb_path)\n        self.collection = self.client.get_or_create_collection(\n            name=settings.seekdb_collection,\n        )\n\n    def _build_document(self, title: str, notes: str, tags: list[str]) -> str:\n        parts = [title]\n        if notes:\n            parts.append(notes)\n        if tags:\n            parts.append(\" \".join(f\"#{t}\" for t in tags))\n        return \"\\n\".join(parts)\n\n    def _build_metadata(\n        self,\n        project_id: str,\n        parameters: dict,\n        metrics: dict,\n        status: str,\n        tags: list[str],\n        date: str,\n    ) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"project_id\": project_id,\n            \"parameters\": json.dumps(parameters, ensure_ascii=False),\n            \"metrics\": json.dumps(metrics, ensure_ascii=False),\n            \"status\": status,\n            \"tags\": json.dumps(tags, ensure_ascii=False),\n            \"date\": date,\n        }\n\n    def add_experiment(\n        self,\n        project_id: str,\n        title: str,\n        parameters: dict,\n        metrics: dict,\n        notes: str,\n        tags: list[str],\n        status: str,\n        date: str | None = None,\n    ) -> str:\n        exp_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        date = date or datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n        document = self._build_document(title, notes, tags)\n        metadata = self._build_metadata(project_id, parameters, metrics, status, tags, date)\n        self.collection.upsert(ids=[exp_id], documents=[document], metadatas=[metadata])\n        return exp_id\n\n    def search(\n        self,\n        query: str,\n        project_id: str | None = None,\n        limit: int = 10,\n    ) -> list[dict]:\n        query_params: dict = {\n            \"query_texts\": query,\n            \"n_results\": limit * 2 if project_id else limit,\n        }\n        results = self.collection.query(**query_params)\n        experiments = []\n        if results and results.get(\"ids\") and results[\"ids\"][0]:\n            for i, exp_id in enumerate(results[\"ids\"][0]):\n                meta = results[\"metadatas\"][0][i] if results[\"metadatas\"] else {}\n                distance = results[\"distances\"][0][i] if results.get(\"distances\") else None\n                doc = results[\"documents\"][0][i] if results.get(\"documents\") else \"\"\n                if project_id and meta.get(\"project_id\") != project_id:\n                    continue\n                experiments.append({\n                    \"id\": exp_id,\n                    \"document\": doc,\n                    \"metadata\": meta,\n                    \"distance\": distance,\n                })\n                if len(experiments) >= limit:\n                    break\n        return experiments\n\n    def get_all_experiments(self, project_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n        query_params: dict = {\"query_texts\": \"experiment\", \"n_results\": 100}\n        results = self.collection.query(**query_params)\n        experiments = []\n        if results and results.get(\"ids\") and results[\"ids\"][0]:\n            for i, exp_id in enumerate(results[\"ids\"][0]):\n                meta = results[\"metadatas\"][0][i] if results[\"metadatas\"] else {}\n                if project_id and meta.get(\"project_id\") != project_id:\n                    continue\n                experiments.append({\n                    \"id\": exp_id,\n                    \"metadata\": meta,\n                    \"document\": results[\"documents\"][0][i] if results.get(\"documents\") else \"\",\n                })\n        return experiments\n\n    def delete_experiment(self, exp_id: str) -> bool:\n        try:\n            self.collection.delete(ids=[exp_id])\n            return True\n        except Exception:\n            return False\n\n\ndb = SeekDB()\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\ouyan\\Desktop\\oceanbase\\powercontext\\qoder\\backend\\app\\database.py"
}

  "content": "import httpx\n\nfrom .config import settings\n\n\nclass PowerContextClient:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.base_url = settings.powercontext_url\n        self.scope_name = settings.powercontext_scope\n        self._scope_id = None\n\n    async def _get_scope_id(self) -> str:\n        if self._scope_id:\n            return self._scope_id\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:\n            resp = await client.get(f\"{self.base_url}/v1/scopes\")\n            resp.raise_for_status()\n            scopes = resp.json()\n            for scope in scopes:\n                if scope.get(\"name\") == self.scope_name:\n                    self._scope_id = scope[\"id\"]\n                    return self._scope_id\n            resp = await client.post(\n                f\"{self.base_url}/v1/scopes\",\n                json={\"name\": self.scope_name, \"description\": \"实验记录本 - 科研项目追踪\"},\n            )\n            resp.raise_for_status()\n            scope = resp.json()\n            self._scope_id = scope[\"id\"]\n            return self._scope_id\n\n    async def remember(self, key: str, content: str, category: str = \"general\") -> dict:\n        scope_id = await self._get_scope_id()\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:\n            resp = await client.post(\n                f\"{self.base_url}/v1/memory/remember\",\n                json={\n                    \"scope_id\": scope_id,\n                    \"key\": key,\n                    \"content\": content,\n                    \"category\": category,\n                },\n            )\n            resp.raise_for_status()\n            return resp.json()\n\n    async def search_memory(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> list[dict]:\n        scope_id = await self._get_scope_id()\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:\n            resp = await client.post(\n                f\"{self.base_url}/v1/atomic-memory/search\",\n                json={\"scope_id\": scope_id, \"query\": query, \"limit\": limit},\n            )\n            resp.raise_for_status()\n            return resp.json()\n\n    async def list_memories(self) -> list[dict]:\n        scope_id = await self._get_scope_id()\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:\n            resp = await client.post(\n                f\"{self.base_url}/v1/atomic-memory/list\",\n                json={\"scope_id\": scope_id},\n            )\n            resp.raise_for_status()\n            return resp.json()\n\n    async def get_context(self) -> dict:\n        scope_id = await self._get_scope_id()\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:\n            resp = await client.post(\n                f\"{self.base_url}/v1/context/prepare\",\n                json={\"scope_id\": scope_id},\n            )\n            resp.raise_for_status()\n            return resp.json()\n\n\npc_client = PowerContextClient()\n",

}

{
  "content": "import json\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\n\nfrom .database import db\nfrom .models import (\n    ExperimentCreate,\n    ExperimentResponse,\n    ProjectCreate,\n    ProjectUpdate,\n    SearchRequest,\n)\nfrom .powercontext import pc_client\n\n\n@asynccontextmanager\nasync def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n    yield\n\n\napp = FastAPI(\n    title=\"实验记录本 API\",\n    description=\"基于 seekdb + PowerContext 的智能实验记录与检索系统\",\n    version=\"1.0.0\",\n    lifespan=lifespan,\n)\n\napp.add_middleware(\n    CORSMiddleware,\n    allow_origins=[\"*\"],\n    allow_credentials=True,\n    allow_methods=[\"*\"],\n    allow_headers=[\"*\"],\n)\n\n\ndef _parse_metadata(meta: dict) -> dict:\n    def _safe_json(value, default):\n        if isinstance(value, str):\n            try:\n                return json.loads(value)\n            except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):\n                return default\n        return value or default\n\n    return {\n        \"id\": meta.get(\"id\", \"\"),\n        \"project_id\": meta.get(\"project_id\", \"\"),\n        \"title\": meta.get(\"title\", \"\"),\n        \"parameters\": _safe_json(meta.get(\"parameters\"), {}),\n        \"metrics\": _safe_json(meta.get(\"metrics\"), {}),\n        \"notes\": meta.get(\"notes\", \"\"),\n        \"tags\": _safe_json(meta.get(\"tags\"), []),\n        \"status\": meta.get(\"status\", \"success\"),\n        \"date\": meta.get(\"date\", \"\"),\n    }\n\n\n# ── 实验记录 ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@app.post(\"/api/experiments\", response_model=ExperimentResponse)\nasync def create_experiment(exp: ExperimentCreate):\n    exp_id = db.add_experiment(\n        project_id=exp.project_id,\n        title=exp.title,\n        parameters=exp.parameters,\n        metrics=exp.metrics,\n        notes=exp.notes,\n        tags=exp.tags,\n        status=exp.status,\n        date=exp.date,\n    )\n    return ExperimentResponse(\n        id=exp_id,\n        project_id=exp.project_id,\n        title=exp.title,\n        parameters=exp.parameters,\n        metrics=exp.metrics,\n        notes=exp.notes,\n        tags=exp.tags,\n        status=exp.status,\n        date=exp.date or \"\",\n    )\n\n\n@app.get(\"/api/experiments\")\nasync def list_experiments(project_id: str | None = None):\n    experiments = db.get_all_experiments(project_id=project_id)\n    return [_parse_metadata(exp.get(\"metadata\", {})) for exp in experiments]\n\n\n@app.delete(\"/api/experiments/{exp_id}\")\nasync def delete_experiment(exp_id: str):\n    success = db.delete_experiment(exp_id)\n    if not success:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Experiment not found\")\n    return {\"status\": \"deleted\"}\n\n\n# ── 语义搜索 (seekdb) ────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@app.post(\"/api/search\")\nasync def search_experiments(req: SearchRequest):\n    results = db.search(query=req.query, project_id=req.project_id, limit=req.limit)\n    experiments = []\n    for r in results:\n        parsed = _parse_metadata(r.get(\"metadata\", {}))\n        parsed[\"distance\"] = r.get(\"distance\")\n        experiments.append(parsed)\n    return experiments\n\n\n# ── 项目管理 (PowerContext) ───────────────────────────────\n\n\n@app.post(\"/api/projects\")\nasync def create_project(project: ProjectCreate):\n    try:\n        result = await pc_client.remember(\n            key=f\"project:{project.name}\",\n            content=f\"项目名称: {project.name}\\n描述: {project.description}\\n目标: {project.goal}\",\n            category=\"project\",\n        )\n        return {\"name\": project.name, \"description\": project.description, \"goal\": project.goal, \"status\": \"active\", \"memory\": result}\n    except Exception as e:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f\"PowerContext 服务不可用: {e}\")\n\n\n@app.get(\"/api/projects\")\nasync def list_projects():\n    try:\n        memories = await pc_client.list_memories()\n        projects = []\n        if isinstance(memories, list):\n            for m in memories:\n                if m.get(\"category\") == \"project\":\n                    projects.append(m)\n        return projects if projects else memories\n    except Exception as e:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f\"PowerContext 服务不可用: {e}\")\n\n\n@app.post(\"/api/projects/{project_name}/update\")\nasync def update_project(project_name: str, update: ProjectUpdate):\n    try:\n        content_parts = [f\"项目: {project_name}\"]\n        if update.status:\n            content_parts.append(f\"状态: {update.status}\")\n        if update.notes:\n            content_parts.append(f\"备注: {update.notes}\")\n        result = await pc_client.remember(\n            key=f\"project:{project_name}:update\",\n            content=\"\\n\".join(content_parts),\n            category=\"project_update\",\n        )\n        return result\n    except Exception as e:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f\"PowerContext 服务不可用: {e}\")\n\n\n@app.get(\"/api/context\")\nasync def get_context():\n    try:\n        context = await pc_client.get_context()\n        return context\n    except Exception as e:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=f\"PowerContext 服务不可用: {e}\")\n\n\n# ── 统计 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@app.get(\"/api/stats\")\nasync def get_stats():\n    experiments = db.get_all_experiments()\n    project_ids = set()\n    for exp in experiments:\n        meta = exp.get(\"metadata\", {})\n        pid = meta.get(\"project_id\")\n        if pid:\n            project_ids.add(pid)\n    return {\n        \"total_experiments\": len(experiments),\n        \"total_projects\": len(project_ids),\n    }\n\n\n@app.get(\"/api/health\")\nasync def health_check():\n    seekdb_ok = True\n    pc_ok = True\n    try:\n        db.get_all_experiments()\n    except Exception:\n        seekdb_ok = False\n    try:\n        await pc_client.list_memories()\n    except Exception:\n        pc_ok = False\n    return {\"seekdb\": seekdb_ok, \"powercontext\": pc_ok}\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\ouyan\\Desktop\\oceanbase\\powercontext\\qoder\\backend\\app\\main.py"
}

{
  "content": "const API_BASE = window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1'\n  ? 'http://localhost:8080'\n  : '';\n\nasync function api(path, options = {}) {\n  const url = `${API_BASE}/api${path}`;\n  const resp = await fetch(url, {\n    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    ...options,\n  });\n  if (!resp.ok) {\n    const error = await resp.text();\n    throw new Error(`API Error ${resp.status}: ${error}`);\n  }\n  return resp.json();\n}\n\nfunction showToast(message, type = 'info') {\n  const existing = document.querySelector('.toast');\n  if (existing) existing.remove();\n\n  const toast = document.createElement('div');\n  toast.className = `toast ${type}`;\n  toast.textContent = message;\n  document.body.appendChild(toast);\n  setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 3000);\n}\n\nfunction formatDate(dateStr) {\n  if (!dateStr) return '';\n  try {\n    const d = new Date(dateStr);\n    return d.toLocaleDateString('zh-CN', { year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });\n  } catch {\n    return dateStr;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction statusLabel(status) {\n  const labels = { success: '成功', failed: '失败', inconclusive: '不确定' };\n  return labels[status] || status;\n}\n\nfunction renderExperimentCard(exp, showCheckbox = false) {\n  const paramsHtml = Object.entries(exp.parameters || {})\n    .map(([k, v]) => `<span>${k}: ${v}</span>`)\n    .join('');\n  const metricsHtml = Object.entries(exp.metrics || {})\n    .map(([k, v]) => `<span>${k}: ${v}</span>`)\n    .join('');\n  const tagsHtml = (exp.tags || [])\n    .map(t => `<span class=\"tag\">${t}</span>`)\n    .join('');\n\n  return `\n    <div class=\"experiment-card ${showCheckbox ? 'search-result' : ''}\" data-id=\"${exp.id}\">\n      ${showCheckbox ? `<input type=\"checkbox\" class=\"compare-checkbox\" value=\"${exp.id}\" title=\"加入对比\">` : ''}\n      ${exp.distance != null ? `<span class=\"relevance\">相关度: ${(1 / (1 + exp.distance)).toFixed(2)}</span>` : ''}\n      <div class=\"exp-header\">\n        <span class=\"exp-title\">${exp.title || '未命名实验'}</span>\n        <span class=\"exp-date\">${formatDate(exp.date)}</span>\n      </div>\n      ${tagsHtml ? `<div class=\"exp-tags\">${tagsHtml}</div>` : ''}\n      <span class=\"status-badge ${exp.status}\">${statusLabel(exp.status)}</span>\n      ${paramsHtml ? `<div class=\"exp-params\"><strong>参数:</strong> ${paramsHtml}</div>` : ''}\n      ${metricsHtml ? `<div class=\"exp-metrics\"><strong>指标:</strong> ${metricsHtml}</div>` : ''}\n      ${exp.notes ? `<div class=\"exp-notes\">${exp.notes}</div>` : ''}\n      <div class=\"exp-actions\">\n        <button class=\"btn btn-danger btn-sm\" onclick=\"deleteExperiment('${exp.id}')\">删除</button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  `;\n}\n\nasync function deleteExperiment(id) {\n  if (!confirm('确定要删除这条实验记录吗？')) return;\n  try {\n    await api(`/experiments/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });\n    showToast('已删除', 'success');\n    if (typeof loadExperiments === 'function') loadExperiments();\n    if (typeof performSearch === 'function' && document.querySelector('.search-box input')?.value) {\n      performSearch();\n    }\n  } catch (e) {\n    showToast('删除失败: ' + e.message, 'error');\n  }\n}\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\ouyan\\Desktop\\oceanbase\\powercontext\\qoder\\frontend\\js\\api.js"
}

  "content": "<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"zh-CN\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n  <title>实验记录 - 实验记录本</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"css/style.css\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header class=\"navbar\">\n    <a href=\"index.html\" class=\"logo\">\n      <svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\">\n        <path d=\"M9 3H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4m6-6h10a2 2 0 012 2v4M9 3v18m0 0h10a2 2 0 002-2V9M9 21H5a2 2 0 01-2-2V9m0 0h18\"/>\n      </svg>\n      实验记录本\n    </a>\n    <nav>\n      <a href=\"index.html\">首页</a>\n      <a href=\"experiments.html\" class=\"active\">实验记录</a>\n      <a href=\"search.html\">语义搜索</a>\n      <a href=\"projects.html\">项目管理</a>\n      <a href=\"compare.html\">实验对比</a>\n    </nav>\n  </header>\n\n  <main class=\"container\">\n    <div class=\"form-card\">\n      <h2>添加实验记录</h2>\n      <form id=\"experiment-form\">\n        <div class=\"form-grid\">\n          <div class=\"form-group\">\n            <label for=\"project_id\">所属项目 *</label>\n            <input type=\"text\" id=\"project_id\" placeholder=\"如：image-classification\" required>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group\">\n            <label for=\"title\">实验标题 *</label>\n            <input type=\"text\" id=\"title\" placeholder=\"如：ResNet50 baseline with data augmentation\" required>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group\">\n            <label for=\"status\">实验状态</label>\n            <select id=\"status\">\n              <option value=\"success\">成功</option>\n              <option value=\"failed\">失败</option>\n              <option value=\"inconclusive\">不确定</option>\n            </select>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group\">\n            <label for=\"tags\">标签（逗号分隔）</label>\n            <input type=\"text\" id=\"tags\" placeholder=\"如：cnn,baseline,augmentation\">\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group full-width\">\n            <label for=\"parameters\">实验参数（JSON 格式）</label>\n            <textarea id=\"parameters\" placeholder='{\"learning_rate\": 0.001, \"batch_size\": 32, \"epochs\": 50, \"model\": \"ResNet50\"}' style=\"min-height: 80px; font-family: monospace;\"></textarea>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group full-width\">\n            <label for=\"metrics\">实验指标（JSON 格式）</label>\n            <textarea id=\"metrics\" placeholder='{\"accuracy\": 0.923, \"loss\": 0.156, \"f1_score\": 0.91}' style=\"min-height: 80px; font-family: monospace;\"></textarea>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group full-width\">\n            <label for=\"notes\">实验笔记</label>\n            <textarea id=\"notes\" placeholder=\"记录实验过程、观察到的现象、可能的改进方向...\"></textarea>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div style=\"margin-top: 1.5rem; display: flex; gap: 0.75rem;\">\n          <button type=\"submit\" class=\"btn btn-primary\">保存实验记录</button>\n          <button type=\"reset\" class=\"btn btn-secondary\">清空</button>\n        </div>\n      </form>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"section-header\">\n      <h2>实验历史</h2>\n      <select id=\"filter-project\" class=\"btn btn-secondary\" style=\"font-size: 0.85rem;\">\n        <option value=\"\">全部项目</option>\n      </select>\n    </div>\n\n    <div id=\"experiment-list\" class=\"experiment-list\">\n      <div class=\"loading\"><div class=\"spinner\"></div>加载中...</div>\n    </div>\n  </main>\n\n  <script src=\"js/api.js\"></script>\n  <script>\n    const form = document.getElementById('experiment-form');\n    const listEl = document.getElementById('experiment-list');\n    const filterEl = document.getElementById('filter-project');\n    let allExperiments = [];\n\n    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      const paramsRaw = document.getElementById('parameters').value.trim();\n      const metricsRaw = document.getElementById('metrics').value.trim();\n      let parameters = {}, metrics = {};\n      try {\n        if (paramsRaw) parameters = JSON.parse(paramsRaw);\n        if (metricsRaw) metrics = JSON.parse(metricsRaw);\n      } catch {\n        showToast('参数或指标 JSON 格式错误', 'error');\n        return;\n      }\n      const tagsRaw = document.getElementById('tags').value.trim();\n      const tags = tagsRaw ? tagsRaw.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];\n\n      const body = {\n        project_id: document.getElementById('project_id').value.trim(),\n        title: document.getElementById('title').value.trim(),\n        parameters,\n        metrics,\n        notes: document.getElementById('notes').value.trim(),\n        tags,\n        status: document.getElementById('status').value,\n      };\n\n      try {\n        await api('/experiments', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) });\n        showToast('实验记录已保存', 'success');\n        form.reset();\n        loadExperiments();\n      } catch (e) {\n        showToast('保存失败: ' + e.message, 'error');\n      }\n    });\n\n    async function loadExperiments() {\n      try {\n        const projectId = filterEl.value;\n        const url = projectId ? `/experiments?project_id=${encodeURIComponent(projectId)}` : '/experiments';\n        allExperiments = await api(url);\n        renderList(allExperiments);\n        updateProjectFilter(allExperiments);\n      } catch {\n        listEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>暂无实验记录</h3><p>在上方表单中添加你的第一条实验记录</p></div>';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function renderList(experiments) {\n      if (!experiments || experiments.length === 0) {\n        listEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>暂无实验记录</h3><p>在上方表单中添加你的第一条实验记录</p></div>';\n        return;\n      }\n      listEl.innerHTML = experiments.map(exp => renderExperimentCard(exp)).join('');\n    }\n\n    function updateProjectFilter(experiments) {\n      const projects = new Set(experiments.map(e => e.project_id).filter(Boolean));\n      const current = filterEl.value;\n      const options = ['<option value=\"\">全部项目</option>'];\n      projects.forEach(p => {\n        options.push(`<option value=\"${p}\" ${p === current ? 'selected' : ''}>${p}</option>`);\n      });\n      filterEl.innerHTML = options.join('');\n    }\n\n    filterEl.addEventListener('change', loadExperiments);\n    loadExperiments();\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\ouyan\\Desktop\\oceanbase\\powercontext\\qoder\\frontend\\experiments.html"
}

margin-bottom: 1.5rem;\">\n      使用自然语言搜索实验记录。seekdb 向量检索会理解你的意图，找到最相关的实验。\n    </p>\n\n    <div class=\"search-box\">\n      <input type=\"text\" id=\"search-input\" placeholder=\"例如：哪些实验的准确率超过了90%？\" autofocus>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-primary\" onclick=\"performSearch()\">搜索</button>\n    </div>\n\n    <div style=\"display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 1.5rem;\">\n      <span style=\"font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-light); line-height: 2;\">试试：</span>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-secondary btn-sm\" onclick=\"quickSearch('learning rate 较小的实验')\">learning rate 较小的实验</button>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-secondary btn-sm\" onclick=\"quickSearch('准确率高的实验')\">准确率高的实验</button>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-secondary btn-sm\" onclick=\"quickSearch('失败的实验及原因')\">失败的实验及原因</button>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-secondary btn-sm\" onclick=\"quickSearch('使用数据增强的实验')\">使用数据增强的实验</button>\n    </div>\n\n    <div id=\"search-results\" class=\"experiment-list\"></div>\n  </main>\n\n  <script src=\"js/api.js\"></script>\n  <script>\n    const searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input');\n    const resultsEl = document.getElementById('search-results');\n\n    searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter') performSearch();\n    });\n\n    function quickSearch(query) {\n      searchInput.value = query;\n      performSearch();\n    }\n\n    async function performSearch() {\n      const query = searchInput.value.trim();\n      if (!query) return;\n\n      resultsEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"loading\"><div class=\"spinner\"></div>seekdb 检索中...</div>';\n\n      try {\n        const results = await api('/search', {\n          method: 'POST',\n          body: JSON.stringify({ query, limit: 20 }),\n        });\n\n        if (!results || results.length === 0) {\n          resultsEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>未找到相关实验</h3><p>试试换个关键词，或先添加一些实验记录</p></div>';\n          return;\n        }\n\n        resultsEl.innerHTML = `\n          <p style=\"color: var(--text-light); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;\">\n            找到 ${results.length} 条相关结果（基于 seekdb 向量检索）\n          </p>\n          ${results.map(exp => renderExperimentCard(exp, true)).join('')}\n        `;\n      } catch (e) {\n        resultsEl.innerHTML = `<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>搜索失败</h3><p>${e.message}</p></div>`;\n      }\n    }\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n",

</form>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"form-card\">\n      <h2>更新项目状态</h2>\n      <form id=\"update-form\">\n        <div class=\"form-grid\">\n          <div class=\"form-group\">\n            <label for=\"update-name\">项目名称 *</label>\n            <input type=\"text\" id=\"update-name\" placeholder=\"要更新的项目名称\" required>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group\">\n            <label for=\"update-status\">新状态</label>\n            <select id=\"update-status\">\n              <option value=\"active\">进行中</option>\n              <option value=\"paused\">暂停</option>\n              <option value=\"completed\">已完成</option>\n            </select>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"form-group full-width\">\n            <label for=\"update-notes\">进展备注</label>\n            <textarea id=\"update-notes\" placeholder=\"记录项目最新进展、关键决策或待办事项...\" style=\"min-height: 60px;\"></textarea>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div style=\"margin-top: 1.5rem;\">\n          <button type=\"submit\" class=\"btn btn-primary\">更新状态</button>\n        </div>\n      </form>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"section-header\">\n      <h2>项目列表</h2>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-secondary btn-sm\" onclick=\"loadProjects()\">刷新</button>\n    </div>\n\n    <div id=\"project-list\" class=\"project-grid\">\n      <div class=\"loading\"><div class=\"spinner\"></div>从 PowerContext 加载...</div>\n    </div>\n  </main>\n\n  <script src=\"js/api.js\"></script>\n  <script>\n    const projectForm = document.getElementById('project-form');\n    const updateForm = document.getElementById('update-form');\n    const projectListEl = document.getElementById('project-list');\n\n    projectForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      const body = {\n        name: document.getElementById('name').value.trim(),\n        description: document.getElementById('description').value.trim(),\n        goal: document.getElementById('goal').value.trim(),\n      };\n      try {\n        await api('/projects', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) });\n        showToast('项目已创建（PowerContext 已记忆）', updateForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      const name = document.getElementById('update-name').value.trim();\n      const body = {\n        status: document.getElementById('update-status').value,\n        notes: document.getElementById('update-notes').value.trim(),\n      };\n      try {\n        await projectListEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"loading\"><div class=\"spinner\"></div>从 PowerContext 加载...</div>';\n      try {\n        const projects = await api('/projects');\n        if (!projects || projects.length === 0) {\n          projectListEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>暂无项目</h3><p>创建你的第一个科研项目，PowerContext 会记住它</p></div>';\n          return;\n        }\n        projectListEl.innerHTML = projects.map(p => {\n          const content = p.content || '';\n          const statusMatch = content.match(/状态:\\s*(active|paused|completed|进行中|暂停|已完成)/);\n          let class=\"project-status ${status}\">${statusLabels[status]}</span>\n              <p style=\"white-space: pre-wrap; font-size: 0.85rem;\">${content}</p>\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }).join('');\n      } catch (e) {\n        projectListEl.innerHTML = `<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>PowerContext 未连接</h3><p>${e.message}</p><p style=\"margin-top: 0.5rem;\">请先启动 PowerContext {
  "content": "<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"zh-CN\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n  <title>实验对比 - 实验记录本</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"css/style.css\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header class=\"navbar\">\n    <a href=\"index.html\" class=\"logo\">\n      <svg viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\">\n        <path d=\"M9 3H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4m6-6h10a2 2 0 012 2v4M9 3v18m0 0h10a2 2 0 002-2V9M9 21H5a2 2 0 01-2-2V9m0 0h18\"/>\n      </svg>\n      实验记录本\n    </a>\n    <nav>\n      <a href=\"index.html\">首页</a>\n      <a href=\"experiments.html\">实验记录</a>\n      <a href=\"search.html\">语义搜索</a>\n      <a href=\"projects.html\">项目管理</a>\n      <a href=\"compare.html\" class=\"active\">实验对比</a>\n    </nav>\n  </header>\n\n  <main class=\"container\">\n    <div class=\"section-header\">\n      <h2>实验对比</h2>\n    </div>\n    <p style=\"color: var(--text-light); margin-bottom: 1.5rem;\">\n      在「语义搜索」页面勾选实验加入对比，或从下方列表中选择。\n    </p>\n\n    <div style=\"display: flex; gap: 1rem; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; flex-wrap: wrap;\">\n      <button class=\"btn btn-primary\" onclick=\"showCompare()\">生成对比表</button>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-secondary\" onclick=\"clearSelection()\">清空选择</button>\n      <span id=\"selected-count\" style=\"line-height: 2.5; font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-light);\">已选 0 个实验</span>\n    </div>\n\n    <div id=\"compare-result\" style=\"margin-bottom: 2rem;\"></div>\n\n    <div class=\"section-header\">\n      <h2>选择实验</h2>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-secondary btn-sm\" onclick=\"loadAll()\">刷新</button>\n    </div>\n\n    <div id=\"experiment-list\" class=\"experiment-list\">\n      <div class=\"loading\"><div class=\"spinner\"></div>加载中...</div>\n    </div>\n  </main>\n\n  <script src=\"js/api.js\"></script>\n  <script>\n    const listEl = document.getElementById('experiment-list');\n    const compareEl = document.getElementById('compare-result');\n    const countEl = document.getElementById('selected-count');\n    let allExperiments = [];\n    let selectedIds = new Set();\n\n    async function loadAll() {\n      try {\n        allExperiments = await api('/experiments');\n        renderList();\n      } catch {\n        listEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>暂无实验记录</h3><p>请先添加实验记录</p></div>';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function renderList() {\n      if (!allExperiments || allExperiments.length === 0) {\n        listEl.innerHTML = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><h3>暂无实验记录</h3></div>';\n        return;\n      }\n      listEl.innerHTML = allExperiments.map(exp => {\n        const checked = selectedIds.has(exp.id) ? 'checked' : '';\n        return `\n          <div class=\"experiment-card\" style=\"display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 1rem;\">\n            <input type=\"checkbox\" class=\"compare-checkbox\" value=\"${exp.id}\" ${checked} onchange=\"toggleSelect('${exp.id}', this.checked)\" style=\"margin-top: 0.25rem; width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: var(--primary);\">\n            <div style=\"flex: 1;\">\n              <div class=\"exp-header\">\n                <span class=\"exp-title\">${exp.title || '未命名实验'}</span>\n                <span class=\"exp-date\">${formatDate(exp.date)}</span>\n              </div>\n              <span class=\"status-badge ${exp.status}\">${statusLabel(exp.status)}</span>\n              <div style=\"display: flex; gap: 1rem; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 0.5rem; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-light);\">\n                ${Object.entries(exp.parameters || {}).map(([k, v]) => `<span style=\"background: var(--bg); padding: 0.2rem 0.5rem; border-radius: 6px;\">${k}: ${v}</span>`).join('')}\n              </div>\n              <div style=\"display: flex; gap: 1rem; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 0.375rem; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-light);\">\n                ${Object.entries(exp.metrics || {}).map(([k, v]) => `<span style=\"background: #ecfdf5; padding: 0.2rem 0.5rem; border-radius: 6px; color: var(--success);\">${k}: ${v}</span>`).join('')}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n      updateCount();\n    }\n\n    function toggleSelect(id, checked) {\n      if (checked) selectedIds.add(id);\n      else selectedIds.delete(id);\n      updateCount();\n    }\n\n    function updateCount() {\n      countEl.textContent = `已选 ${selectedIds.size} 个实验`;\n    }\n\n    function clearSelection() {\n      selectedIds.clear();\n      renderList();\n      compareEl.innerHTML = '';\n    }\n\n    function showCompare() {\n      if (selectedIds.size < 2) {\n        showToast('请至少选择 2 个实验进行对比', 'error');\n        return;\n      }\n      const selected = allExperiments.filter(e => selectedIds.has(e.id));\n      const allParamKeys = new Set();\n      const allMetricKeys = new Set();\n      selected.forEach(e => {\n        Object.keys(e.parameters || {}).forEach(k => allParamKeys.add(k));\n        Object.keys(e.metrics || {}).forEach(k => allMetricKeys.add(k));\n      });\n\n      let html = '<table class=\"compare-table\"><thead><tr><th>维度</th>';\n      selected.forEach(e => {\n        html += `<th>${e.title || '未命名'}</th>`;\n      });\n      html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';\n\n      html += '<tr><td><strong>项目</strong></td>';\n      selected.forEach(e => { html += `<td>${e.project_id || '-'}</td>`; });\n      html += '</tr>';\n\n      html += '<tr><td><strong>状态</strong></td>';\n      selected.forEach(e => { html += `<td><span class=\"status-badge ${e.status}\">${statusLabel(e.status)}</span></td>`; });\n      html += '</tr>';\n\n      html += '<tr><td><strong>日期</strong></td>';\n      selected.forEach(e => { html += `<td>${formatDate(e.date)}</td>`; });\n      html += '</tr>';\n\n      for (const key of allParamKeys) {\n        html += `<tr><td><strong>参数: ${key}</strong></td>`;\n        selected.forEach(e => {\n          const val = e.parameters?.[key];\n          html += `<td>${val != null ? val : '-'}</td>`;\n        });\n        html += '</tr>';\n      }\n\n      for (const key of allMetricKeys) {\n        html += `<tr><td><strong>指标: ${key}</strong></td>`;\n        const values = selected.map(e => e.metrics?.[key]);\n        const numericValues = values.filter(v => typeof v === 'number');\n        const maxVal = numericValues.length > 0 ? Math.max(...numericValues) : null;\n        selected.forEach((e, i) => {\n          const val = e.metrics?.[key];\n          const isMax = val != null && val === maxVal && numericValues.length > 1;\n          html += `<td ${isMax ? 'style=\"color: var(--success); font-weight: 600;\"' : ''}>${val != null ? val : '-'}${isMax ? ' ★' : ''}</td>`;\n        });\n        html += '</tr>';\n      }\n\n      html += '</tbody></table>';\n      compareEl.innerHTML = html;\n      compareEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });\n    }\n\n    loadAll();\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\ouyan\\Desktop\\oceanbase\\powercontext\\qoder\\frontend\\compare.html"
}

```json
{
  "content": "# 实验记录本 (Lab Notebook)\n\n基于 **seekdb** 向量检索 + **PowerContext** 项目记忆的智能实验记录与检索系统。\n\n## 功能特性\n\n- **实验记录** — 结构化记录每次实验的参数、指标、结果和分析笔记\n- **语义搜索** — 用自然语言搜索实验历史，seekdb 向量检索秒级定位相关实验\n- **项目追踪** — PowerContext 记住每个科研项目的目标、状态和关键决策，跨会话持久化\n- **实验对比** — 选择多个实验，自动生成参数与结果对比表，快速找到最优配置\n\n## 技术架构\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Frontend (Static HTML/CSS/JS)                   │\n│  首页 | 实验记录 | 语义搜索 | 项目管理 | 实验对比  │\n└──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────┘\n                       │ HTTP\n┌──────────────────────▼──────────────────────────┐\n│  Backend (Python FastAPI)                        │\n│  /api/experiments  /api/search  /api/projects    │\n└────────┬─────────────────────────────┬──────────┘\n         │                             │\n    ┌────▼────┐                  ┌─────▼─────┐\n    │ seekdb  │                  │PowerContext│\n    │ (pyseekdb)│                │ (HTTP API) │\n    │ 向量检索  │                  │ 项目记忆    │\n    └─────────┘                  └───────────┘\n```\n\n### 技术分工\n\n| 组件 | 职责 |\n|:---|:---|\n| **seekdb (pyseekdb)** | 存储实验记录的文本 embedding；提供语义相似度检索；支持按项目过滤 |\n| **PowerContext** | 管理科研项目元信息（目标、状态、关键决策）；跨会话记忆持久化；提供上下文感知的项目追踪 |\n\n## 快速开始\n\n### 前置条件\n\n- Python 3.11+\n- Node.js 18+（仅开发时需要）\n- PowerContext 服务（可选，项目追踪功能依赖）\n\n### 1. 安装后端依赖\n\n```bash\ncd backend\npython -m venv .venv\nsource .venv/bin/activate  # Windows: .venv\\Scripts\\activate\npip install -r requirements.txt\n```\n\n### 2. 配置环境变量\n\n```bash\ncp .env.example .env\n# 编辑 .env 文件，配置 seekdb 路径和 PowerContext 地址\n```\n\n### 3. 启动 PowerContext（可选）\n\n```bash\n# 如果尚未安装 PowerContext\nuv tool install --force \"powercontext[cli,server]==1.4.1\"\npowercontext config init --language zh --output .env\npowercontext server run --env-file .env\n```\n\n### 4. 启动后端服务\n\n```bash\ncd backend\npython run.py\n# 服务启动在 http://localhost:8080\n```\n\n### 5. 打开前端\n\n直接用浏览器打开 `frontend/index.html`，或使用任意静态文件服务器：\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\npython -m http.server 3000\n# 访问 http://localhost:3000\n```\n\n## 验证样例\n\n详见 [docs/validation.md](docs/validation.md)。\n\n## 项目结构\n\n```\nqoder/\n├── README.md                    # 本文件\n├── backend/\n│   ├── requirements.txt         # Python 依赖\n│   ├── .env.example             # 环境变量模板\n│   ├── run.py                   # 启动入口\n│   └── app/\n│       ├── config.py            # 配置管理\n│       ├── database.py          # seekdb (pyseekdb) 集成\n│       ├── powercontext.py      # PowerContext 客户端\n│       ├── models.py            # 数据模型\n│       └── main.py              # FastAPI 应用\n├── frontend/\n│   ├── index.html               # {
  "content": "# 运行说明\n\n## 环境要求\n\n| 组件 | 版本要求 | 说明 |\n|:---|:---|:---|\n| Python | 3.11+ | 后端运行时 |\n| seekdb (pyseekdb) | >= 0.1.0 | 向量检索（嵌入式模式） |\n| PowerContext | 1.4.1 | 项目记忆（可选，通过 HTTP API 接入） |\n\n## 安装步骤\n\n### 1. 克隆仓库\n\n```bash\ngit clone <repository-url>\ncd qoder\n```\n\n### 2. 安装后端依赖\n\n```bash\ncd backend\npython -m venv .venv\n\n# Linux/macOS\nsource .venv/bin/activate\n# Windows\n.venv\\Scripts\\activate\n\npip install -r requirements.txt\n```\n\n### 3. 配置环境变量\n\n```bash\ncp .env.example .env\n```\n\n编辑 `.env` 文件：\n\n```ini\n# seekdb 嵌入式数据库路径\nSEEKDB_PATH=./data/experiments.db\n\n# PowerContext 服务地址（如果已启动）\nPOWERCONTEXT_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000\nPOWERCONTEXT_SCOPE=lab-notebook\n\n# 后端服务端口\nHOST=0.0.0.0\nPORT=8080\n```\n\n### 4. 启动 PowerContext 服务（可选）\n\n项目追踪功能依赖 PowerContext。如果不需要该功能，可以跳过此步骤。\n\n```bash\n# 安装 PowerContext\nuv tool install --force \"powercontext[cli,server]==1.4.1\"\n\n# 初始化配置\npowercontext config init --language zh --output .env\n\n# 启动服务\npowercontext server run --env-file .env\n```\n\nPowerContext 默认运行在 `http://127.0.0.1:8000`。\n\n### 5. 启动后端服务\n\n```bash\ncd backend\npython run.py\n```\n\n后端启动后会打印：\n```\nINFO:     Uvicorn running on http://0.0.0.0:8080\n```\n\n### 6. 启动前端\n\n**方式一：直接打开文件**\n\n用浏览器打开 `frontend/index.html`。\n\n**方式二：使用静态文件服务器**\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\npython -m http.server 3000\n# 访问 http://localhost:3000\n```\n\n## 部署到 Qoder Sites\n\n本项目前端为纯静态文件，可直接部署到 Qoder Sites：\n\n1. 在 Qoder 中创建 Sites 项目\n2. 将 `frontend/` 目录下的所有文件上传\n3. 配置后端 API 地址（修改 `frontend/js/api.js` 中的 `API_BASE`）\n4. 发布站点\n\n## 故障排查\n\n### seekdb 初始化失败\n\n- 确保 `SEEKDB_PATH` 目录可写\n- 首次启动会自动创建数据库文件\n\n### PowerContext 连接失败\n\n- 检查 PowerContext 服务是否已启动：`curl http://127.0.0.1:8000/health`\n- 确认 `.env` 中的 `POWERCONTEXT_URL` 地址正确\n- 项目追踪功能会降级为不可用，其他功能不受影响\n\n### 前端无法连接后端\n\n- 确保后端运行在 `http://localhost:8080`\n- 检查浏览器控制台是否有 CORS 错误\n- 如果部署到 Qoder Sites，需要修改 `api.js` 中的 `API_BASE` 为后端实际地址\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\ouyan\\Desktop\\oceanbase\\powercontext\\qoder\\docs\\run.md"
}

`frontend/index.html`）\n- PowerContext（可选）：`http://127.0.0.1:8000`\n\n---\n\n## 验证样例 1：实验记录 + 语义搜索（seekdb）\n\n### 目标\n\n验证 seekdb 向量检索能够理解自然语言查询，找到语义相关的实验记录。\n\n### 步骤\n\n#### 1.1 录入实验数据\n\n在「实验记录」页面（`experiments.html`），依次添加以下 5 条实验记录：\n\n**实验 1：ResNet50 baseline**\n- 所属项目：`image-classification`\n- 标题：`ResNet50 baseline without augmentation`\n- 状态：成功\n- 标签：`cnn,baseline`\n- 参数：`{\"model\": \"ResNet50\", \"learning_rate\": 0.01, \"batch_size\": 32, \"epochs\": 50}`\n- 指标：`{\"accuracy\": 0.856, \"loss\": 0.423}`\n- 笔记：`基础模型，未使用数据增强。训练过程稳定，但准确率偏低，存在过拟合迹象。`\n\n**实验 2：ResNet50 with augmentation**\n- 所属项目：`image-classification`\n- 标题：`ResNet50 with data augmentation`\n- 状态：成功\n- 标签：`cnn,augmentation`\n- 参数：`{\"model\": \"ResNet50\", \"learning_rate\": 0.005, \"batch_size\": 64, \"epochs\": 80}`\n- 指标：`{\"accuracy\": 0.923, \"loss\": 0.215}`\n- 笔记：`加入随机翻转和裁剪增强。准确率显著提升，过拟合缓解。`\n\n**实验 3：VGG16 comparison**\n- 所属项目：`image-classification`\n- 标题：`VGG16 with augmentation`\n- 状态：成功\n- 标签：`cnn,augmentation,vgg`\n- 参数：`{\"model\": \"VGG16\", \"learning_rate\": 0.001, \"batch_size\": 32, \"epochs\": 100}`\n- 指标：`{\"accuracy\": 0.891, \"loss\": 0.312}`\n- 笔记：`VGG16 比 ResNet50 慢，准确率也略低，参数量更大但效果不如 ResNet。`\n\n**实验 4：Low learning rate experiment**\n- 所属项目：`image-classification`\n- 标题：`ResNet50 with very low learning rate`\n- 状态：不确定\n- 标签：`cnn,learning-rate`\n- 参数：`{\"model\": \"ResNet50\", \"learning_rate\": 0.0001, \"batch_size\": 32, \"epochs\": 50}`\n- 指标：`{\"accuracy\": 0.782, \"loss\": 0.567}`\n- 笔记：`学习率过低导致收敛缓慢，50 个 epoch 后仍未收敛。需要更多 epoch 或提高学习率。`\n\n**实验 5：Failed experiment**\n- 所属项目：`image-classification`\n- 标题：`ResNet50 with too high learning rate`\n- 状态：失败\n- 标签：`cnn,learning-rate,failed`\n- 参数：`{\"model\": \"ResNet50\", \"learning_rate\": 0.1, \"batch_size\": 32, \"epochs\": 30}`\n- 指标：`{\"accuracy\": 0.234, \"loss\": 2.891}`\n- 笔记：`学习率过高导致 loss 爆炸，训练完全失败。需要降低学习率或使用学习率调度器。`\n\n#### 1.2 语义搜索验证\n\n在「语义搜索」页面（`search.html`），依次输入以下查询：\n\n**查询 1：** `哪些实验的准确率超过了90%？`\n\n预期结果：实验 2（accuracy: 0.923）排在最前面。seekdb 向量检索理解\"准确率超过90%\"的语义，匹配到笔记和指标中相关的实验。\n\n**查询 2：** `学习率较低的实验`\n\n预期结果：实验 4（learning_rate: 0.0001）排在最前面。seekdb 理解\"学习率较低\"的语义，匹配到相关实验。\n\n**查询 3：** `失败的实验及原因`\n\n预期结果：实验 5（status: failed）排在最前面。seekdb 匹配到笔记中\"训练完全失败\"的描述。\n\n**查询 4：** `使用数据增强的实验`\n\n预期结果：实验 2 和实验 3 排在前面。seekdb 匹配到标签 `augmentation` 和笔记中的\"数据增强\"。\n\n### 验证要点\n\n- seekdb 的向量检索能够理解自然语言查询的语义\n- 检索结果按语义相关度排序（显示相关度分数）\n- 检索结果包含完整的实验信息（参数、指标、笔记）\n\n---\n\n## 验证样例 2：项目追踪（PowerContext）\n\n### 目标\n\n验证 PowerContext 能够持久化项目信息，跨会话记忆。\n\n### 步骤\n\n#### 2.1 创建项目\n\n在「项目管理」页面（`projects.html`）：\n\n1. 填写项目信息：\n   - 项目名称：`image-classification`\n   - 研究目标：`提升 ImageNet 分类准确率至 95%`\n   - 项目描述：`基于 ResNet 架构，探索数据增强和学习率调度对分类性能的影响。`\n\n2. 点击「创建项目」\n\n预期结果：提示\"项目已创建（PowerContext 已记忆）\"。\n\n#### 2.2 更新项目状态\n\n1. 在「更新项目状态」表单中：\n   - 项目名称：`image-classification`\n   - 新状态：`进行中`\n   - 进展备注：`已完成 5 组实验，ResNet50 + augmentation 效果最佳（92.3%），继续探索学习率调度。`\n\n2. 点击「更新状态」\n\n预期结果：提示\"项目状态已更新\"。\n\n#### 2.3 验证持久化\n\n1. 刷新页面\n2. 点击「刷新」按钮重新加载项目列表\n\n预期结果：项目信息仍然存在，包含最新的状态更新。PowerContext 的记忆是持久化的。\n\n#### 2.4 跨会话验证（可选）\n\n1. 关闭后端服务，重新启动\n2. 再次打开项目管理页面\n\n预期结果：项目信息仍然存在。PowerContext 的记忆不随服务重启而丢失。\n\n### 验证要点\n\n- PowerContext 成功存储项目信息（通过 HTTP API）\n- 项目信息持久化，刷新/重启后仍然存在\n- 可以更新项目状态，PowerContext 记录变更历史\n\n---\n\n## 验证样例 3：实验对比\n\n### 目标\n\n验证实验对比功能，展示参数与结果的对比表。\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. 在「实验对比」页面（`compare.html`），勾选实验 1、2、4、5\n2. 点击「生成对比表」\n\n预期结果：\n\n| 维度 | ResNet50 baseline | ResNet50 + aug | Low LR | 在项目中发挥的作用 | 验证方式 |\n|:---|:---|:---|\n| **seekdb** | 存储实验记录 embedding，提供语义相似度检索 | 搜索\"准确率超过90%\"能找到对应实验 |\n| **PowerContext** | 持久化项目信息，跨会话记忆 | 刷新/重启后项目信息仍在 |\n",

1	# Execute Sites operations
2	
3	Use the `qoder_sites` MCP tools for all Sites operations. Pass the operation's JSON input directly to its tool; discover its schema through the host's MCP tool discovery when needed.
4	
5	In examples, `sites <operation>` means the MCP tool named `<operation>`. The host provides the current account and trusted session directory. Use `get_context` to read them; never supply credentials, a session ID or an API origin yourself.
6	
7	After checking publication status, use `show_publish_confirmation` to open the frozen Desktop preview. If the Sites MCP tools are unavailable, report the host/plugin loading failure and preserve the request for retry.
8

#### Tool: Read
